

// ==========================================
// File: src/App.tsx
// ==========================================
import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route } from "react-router-dom";
import './App.css';

// 1. 메인 홈
import { HomePage } from "./pages/HomePage";

// 2. 인증/회원 (auth)
import { LoginPage } from "./pages/auth/LoginPage";
import { SignupPage } from "./pages/auth/SignupPage";
import { FindPasswordPage } from "./pages/auth/FindPasswordPage";
import { ProfileEditPage } from "./pages/auth/ProfileEditPage";
import { KakaoCallback } from "./pages/auth/KakaoCallback";

// 3. 숙소 (stay)
import { StayDetailPage } from "./pages/stay/StayDetailPage";
import { WishlistPage } from "./pages/stay/WishlistPage";

// 4. 관광지 (tour)
import { TourDetailPage } from "./pages/tour/TourDetailPage";
import { TourWishlistPage } from "./pages/tour/TourWishlistPage";

// 5. 예약/결제 (reservation)
import { ReservationPage } from "./pages/reservation/ReservationPage";
import { PaymentSuccessPage } from "./pages/reservation/PaymentSuccessPage";
import { MyReservationsPage } from "./pages/reservation/MyReservationsPage";

// 6. 호스트 파트너 센터 (host)
import { HostAccommodationPage } from "./pages/host/HostAccommodationPage";
import { HostReservationsPage } from "./pages/host/HostReservationsPage";
import { HostReviewsPage } from "./pages/host/HostReviewsPage";

// 7. 총괄 관리자 센터 (admin)
import { AdminAccommodationsPage } from "./pages/admin/AdminAccommodationsPage";

import { AdminPartnersPage } from "./pages/admin/AdminPartnersPage";
import { setupAxiosInterceptors } from './api/axiosInterceptor';

import { ChatWidget } from './components/common/ChatWidget';

// 💡 2. App 밖에서 한 번만 실행되도록 호출 (이 한 줄로 모든 axios가 감시를 받습니다!)
setupAxiosInterceptors();

function App() {
    return (
        <BrowserRouter>
            <Routes>
                {/* 메인 홈 */}
                <Route path="/" element={<HomePage />} />

                {/* 인증/회원 */}
                <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
                <Route path="/signup" element={<SignupPage />} />
                <Route path="/find-password" element={<FindPasswordPage />} />
                <Route path="/profile" element={<ProfileEditPage />} />
                <Route path="/oauth/callback" element={<KakaoCallback />} />

                {/* 숙소 */}
                <Route path="/stays/:contentId" element={<StayDetailPage />} />
                <Route path="/wishlist" element={<WishlistPage />} />

                {/* 관광지 */}
                <Route path="/tours/:contentId" element={<TourDetailPage />} />
                <Route path="/tour-wishlist" element={<TourWishlistPage />} />

                {/* 예약/결제 */}
                <Route path="/reservation" element={<ReservationPage />} />
                <Route path="/payment/success" element={<PaymentSuccessPage />} />
                <Route path="/my-reservations" element={<MyReservationsPage />} />

                {/* 호스트 파트너 센터 */}
                <Route path="/host/accommodation" element={<HostAccommodationPage />} />
                <Route path="/host/reservations" element={<HostReservationsPage />} />
                <Route path="/host/reviews" element={<HostReviewsPage />} />

                {/* 총괄 관리자 센터 */}
                <Route path="/admin/accommodations" element={<AdminAccommodationsPage />} />
                {/* 💡 새로운 파트너 관리 페이지 라우트 추가 */}
                <Route path="/admin/partners" element={<AdminPartnersPage />} />
            </Routes>
            <ChatWidget />
        </BrowserRouter>
    );
}

export default App;

import App from "./App";
import "./style.css";

createRoot(document.getElementById("root")!).render(<App/>);

// File: src/types/stay.ts

/**
 * [테이블 5] ACCOMMODATION + JPA DTO 응답 규격
 */
export type  Stay= {
  contentId: string;       // PK, 식별자 (기존 id 대신 사용)
  memberId?: number | null;
  title: string;           // 숙소명 (기존 name 대신 사용)
  lodgingType?: string;    // AC01 ~ AC06 코드 또는 숙박 유형
  tel?: string;
  zipcode?: string;
  addr?: string;           // 기본 주소 (기존 address 대신 사용)
  checkinTime?: string;
  checkoutTime?: string;
  roomCount?: string;
  roomType?: string;
  parking?: string;
  subFacility?: string;
  overview?: string;       // 숙소 개요
  firstImage?: string;     // 대표 이미지 URL
  firstImage2?: string;    // 서브 썸네일 URL
  mapX?: string;
  mapY?: string;
  basePrice: number;       // 객실 최저가 (원화)
  remainingRooms?: number; // 잔여 객실 수
};

export type Room = {
  roomId: number;
  contentId: string;
  roomTitle?: string;
  roomSize?: string;
  roomBaseCount?: string;
  roomMaxCount?: string;
  pricePerNight?: number;
};

export type Booking = {
  contentId: string;
  roomId?: number;
  checkIn: string;
  checkOut: string;
  guests: number;
  guestName: string;
  phone: string;
};

export type BookingResponse = {
  reservationCode?: string;
  id?: number;
  status?: string;
  message?: string;
};


// ==========================================
// File: src/components/home/HostApplyBanner.tsx
// ==========================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { requestApplyHost } from '../../api/auth.ts';
import axios from 'axios';
import { X, Building } from 'lucide-react';

export function HostApplyBanner() {
    const [userRole, setUserRole] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [businessNo, setBusinessNo] = useState('');
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (token) {
            try {
                const base64Payload = token.split('.')[1];
                const decodedPayload = JSON.parse(
                    decodeURIComponent(
                        atob(base64Payload)
                            .split('')
                            .map((c) => '%' + ('00' + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16)).slice(-2))
                            .join('')
                    )
                );
                // 토큰에서 역할 추출
                setUserRole(decodedPayload.role || null);
            } catch {
                setUserRole(null);
            }
        } else {
            setUserRole(null);
        }
    }, []);

    // 💡 로그인하지 않았거나, 일반 사용자(ROLE_USER)가 아니면 화면에 아예 그리지 않음
    if (userRole !== 'ROLE_USER') {
        return null;
    }

    const handleApply = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!businessNo.trim()) {
            setErrorMessage('사업자등록번호를 입력해주세요.');
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);
        setErrorMessage('');

        try {
            const data = await requestApplyHost(businessNo.trim());
            localStorage.setItem('accessToken', data.token);
            alert('숙소 관리자 신청이 완료되었습니다! 파트너 권한으로 전환됩니다.');
            setIsModalOpen(false);
            window.location.reload();
        } catch (err: unknown) {
            if (axios.isAxiosError(err) && err.response) {
                setErrorMessage(err.response.data?.message || '신청 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
            } else {
                setErrorMessage('서버와 통신할 수 없습니다.');
            }
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return (

        // 숙소 관리자 신청
        <section style={{ backgroundColor: '#0f333f', padding: '24px 0', borderTop: '1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)' }}>
            <div className="container" style={{
                display: 'grid',
                gridTemplateColumns: '1fr auto 1fr', // 💡 좌(비움) : 중앙(문구) : 우(버튼) 3분할
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '16px'
            }}>
                {/* 1. 좌측 균형을 맞추기 위한 빈 영역 */}
                <div />

                {/* 2. 중앙으로 배치되는 문구 영역 */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
                    <div style={{
                        width: '36px',
                        height: '36px',
                        borderRadius: '8px',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(110, 231, 183, 0.15)',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center'
                    }}>
                        <Building size={20} color="#6ee7b7" />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <strong style={{ display: 'block', color: '#ffffff', fontSize: '15px' }}>
                            숙소를 신청하고 싶은가요?
                        </strong>
                        <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '13px' }}>
                파트너로 등록하고 더 많은 여행객에게 숙소를 소개해 보세요.
            </span>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 3. 우측 끝에 고정되는 버튼 영역 */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(true)}
                        style={{
                            padding: '10px 22px',
                            fontSize: '14px',
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            color: '#123f4d',
                            backgroundColor: '#6ee7b7',
                            border: 'none',
                            borderRadius: '24px',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(110, 231, 183, 0.2)',
                        }}
                    >
                        숙소관리자 신청
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* 사업자번호 입력 모달창 */}
            {isModalOpen && (
                <div style={{
                    position: 'fixed',
                    top: 0,
                    left: 0,
                    width: '100vw',
                    height: '100vh',
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55)',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                    zIndex: 2000
                }}>
                    <div style={{
                        width: '90%',
                        maxWidth: '400px',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '16px',
                        padding: '28px',
                        boxShadow: '0 20px 25px -5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2)',
                        position: 'relative'
                    }}>
                        <button
                            onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                            style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '18px', right: '18px', background: 'none', border: 'none', cursor: 'pointer' }}
                        >
                            <X size={20} color="#64748b" />
                        </button>

                        <h3 style={{ margin: '0 0 8px 0', fontSize: '18px', color: '#0f172a' }}>숙소 관리자(호스트) 신청</h3>
                        <p style={{ margin: '0 0 20px 0', fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b' }}>
                            운영 중이신 사업자등록번호를 입력하시면 즉시 호스트 권한이 부여됩니다.
                        </p>

                        {errorMessage && (
                            <div style={{ padding: '8px 12px', backgroundColor: '#fee2e2', color: '#dc2626', borderRadius: '6px', fontSize: '12px', marginBottom: '14px' }}>
                                {errorMessage}
                            </div>
                        )}

                        <form onSubmit={handleApply}>
                            <div style={{ marginBottom: '18px' }}>
                                <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#334155', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                    사업자등록번호
                                </label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    required
                                    value={businessNo}
                                    onChange={(e) => setBusinessNo(e.target.value)}
                                    placeholder="000-00-00000"
                                    style={{
                                        width: '100%',
                                        padding: '10px 12px',
                                        border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                        borderRadius: '8px',
                                        boxSizing: 'border-box',
                                        fontSize: '14px',
                                    }}
                                />
                            </div>

                            <button
                                type="submit"
                                disabled={loading}
                                style={{
                                    width: '100%',
                                    padding: '11px',
                                    backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                    color: '#ffffff',
                                    border: 'none',
                                    borderRadius: '8px',
                                    fontWeight: 600,
                                    fontSize: '14px',
                                    cursor: loading ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer'
                                }}
                            >
                                {loading ? '신청 진행 중...' : '신청 완료'}
                            </button>
                        </form>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </section>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/components/home/HomeNavbar.tsx
// ==========================================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
    ArrowRight,
    User,
    ChevronDown,
    Heart,
    Map as MapIcon,
    CalendarCheck,
    Settings,
    LogOut,
    Home,
    MessageSquare,
    FileCheck,
    PlusCircle,
    Users
} from 'lucide-react';
import { WeatherWidget} from "../common/WeatherWidget.tsx";
import { NotificationBell} from "../common/NotificationBell.tsx";

interface HomeNavbarProps {
    isLoggedIn: boolean;
    onLogout: () => void;
    onOpenTourMap: () => void;
}

export function HomeNavbar({ isLoggedIn, onLogout, onOpenTourMap }: HomeNavbarProps) {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [isDropdownOpen, setIsDropdownOpen] = useState(false);
    const [userName, setUserName] = useState('내 계정');
    const [userRole, setUserRole] = useState('ROLE_USER');
    const [hasApprovedAccommodation, setHasApprovedAccommodation] = useState(false);
    const dropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

    // 토큰 기반 닉네임 및 권한 파싱
    useEffect(() => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (token && isLoggedIn) {
            try {
                const base64Payload = token.split('.')[1];
                const decodedPayload = JSON.parse(
                    decodeURIComponent(
                        atob(base64Payload)
                            .split('')
                            .map((c) => '%' + ('00' + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16)).slice(-2))
                            .join('')
                    )
                );
                if (decodedPayload.name) {
                    setUserName(decodedPayload.name);
                } else if (decodedPayload.sub) {
                    setUserName(decodedPayload.sub.split('@')[0]);
                }
                if (decodedPayload.role) {
                    setUserRole(decodedPayload.role);
                }
            } catch {
                setUserName('Unknown');
            }
        }
    }, [isLoggedIn]);

    // 호스트 계정 승인 숙소 보유 여부 체크
    useEffect(() => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (token && isLoggedIn && userRole === 'ROLE_HOST') {
            fetch('/api/v1/accommodations/has-approved', {
                headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
            })
                .then((res) => (res.ok ? res.json() : false))
                .then((data: boolean) => setHasApprovedAccommodation(data))
                .catch(() => setHasApprovedAccommodation(false));
        }
    }, [isLoggedIn, userRole]);

    // 드롭다운 외부 클릭 감지
    useEffect(() => {
        const handleClickOutside = (event: MouseEvent) => {
            if (dropdownRef.current && !dropdownRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
                setIsDropdownOpen(false);
            }
        };
        document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
        return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    }, []);

    const handleLogoutClick = () => {
        setIsDropdownOpen(false);
        onLogout();
    };

    return (
        <header className="header">
            <div className="nav container">
                <a className="brand" href="#top" aria-label="제주 야호 홈">
                    <img className="brand-mark" src="/favicon.png" alt="제주 야호 심볼" />
                    <span>
                        제주 야호<small>JEJU YA-HO</small>
                    </span>
                </a>

                <nav className="nav-links" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '28px' }}>
                    <a href="#destinations" style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', lineHeight: 1 }}>
                        추천 숙소
                    </a>
                    <a href="#travel" style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', lineHeight: 1 }}>
                        추천 관광지
                    </a>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={onOpenTourMap}
                        style={{
                            background: 'none',
                            border: 'none',
                            font: 'inherit',
                            color: 'inherit',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            padding: 0,
                            display: 'inline-flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            lineHeight: 1,
                            verticalAlign: 'middle'
                        }}
                    >
                        관광 지도
                    </button>
                    <WeatherWidget />
                </nav>

                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px' }}>
                    <a className="nav-cta" href="#stays">
                        제주 숙소 둘러보기 <ArrowRight size={16} />
                    </a>

                    {isLoggedIn ? (
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' }}>
                            <div style={{ position: 'relative' }} ref={dropdownRef}>
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setIsDropdownOpen((prev) => !prev)}
                                    style={{
                                        display: 'flex',
                                        alignItems: 'center',
                                        gap: '8px',
                                        padding: '6px 14px',
                                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                                        border: '1.5px solid #123f4d',
                                        borderRadius: '24px',
                                        cursor: 'pointer',
                                        color: '#123f4d',
                                        fontWeight: 600,
                                        fontSize: '14px',
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <div style={{
                                        width: '26px',
                                        height: '26px',
                                        borderRadius: '50%',
                                        backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                        color: '#ffffff',
                                        display: 'flex',
                                        alignItems: 'center',
                                        justifyContent: 'center'
                                    }}>
                                        <User size={16} />
                                    </div>
                                    <span>{userName}</span>

                                    {userRole === 'ROLE_HOST' && (
                                        <span style={{
                                            fontSize: '11px',
                                            backgroundColor: '#e6f4ea',
                                            color: '#137333',
                                            padding: '2px 7px',
                                            borderRadius: '10px',
                                            fontWeight: 500
                                        }}>
                                            숙소관리자(파트너)
                                        </span>
                                    )}
                                    {userRole === 'ROLE_ADMIN' && (
                                        <span style={{
                                            fontSize: '11px',
                                            backgroundColor: '#fce8e6',
                                            color: '#c5221f',
                                            padding: '2px 7px',
                                            borderRadius: '10px',
                                            fontWeight: 500
                                        }}>
                                            총괄 관리자
                                        </span>
                                    )}

                                    <ChevronDown size={14} style={{
                                        transform: isDropdownOpen ? 'rotate(180deg)' : 'none',
                                        transition: '0.2s'
                                    }} />
                                </button>

                                {isDropdownOpen && (
                                    <div
                                        style={{
                                            position: 'absolute',
                                            top: '115%',
                                            right: 0,
                                            width: '210px',
                                            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                                            borderRadius: '12px',
                                            boxShadow: '0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12)',
                                            border: '1px solid #f0f0f0',
                                            padding: '8px 0',
                                            zIndex: 1000,
                                            display: 'flex',
                                            flexDirection: 'column',
                                        }}
                                    >
                                        {userRole === 'ROLE_USER' && (
                                            <>
                                                <button
                                                    type="button"
                                                    onClick={() => {
                                                        setIsDropdownOpen(false);
                                                        navigate('/wishlist');
                                                    }}
                                                    style={dropdownItemStyle}
                                                >
                                                    <Heart size={16} /> 숙소 위시리스트
                                                </button>
                                                <button
                                                    type="button"
                                                    onClick={() => {
                                                        setIsDropdownOpen(false);
                                                        navigate('/tour-wishlist');
                                                    }}
                                                    style={dropdownItemStyle}
                                                >
                                                    <MapIcon size={16} /> 관광지 위시리스트
                                                </button>
                                                <button
                                                    type="button"
                                                    onClick={() => {
                                                        setIsDropdownOpen(false);
                                                        navigate('/my-reservations');
                                                    }}
                                                    style={dropdownItemStyle}
                                                >
                                                    <CalendarCheck size={16} /> 예매한 숙소 조회
                                                </button>
                                            </>
                                        )}

                                        {userRole === 'ROLE_HOST' && (
                                            <>
                                                {hasApprovedAccommodation ? (
                                                    <>
                                                        <button
                                                            type="button"
                                                            onClick={() => {
                                                                setIsDropdownOpen(false);
                                                                navigate('/host/reservations');
                                                            }}
                                                            style={dropdownItemStyle}
                                                        >
                                                            <CalendarCheck size={16} /> 예약 내역 관리
                                                        </button>
                                                        <button
                                                            type="button"
                                                            onClick={() => {
                                                                setIsDropdownOpen(false);
                                                                navigate('/host/accommodation');
                                                            }}
                                                            style={dropdownItemStyle}
                                                        >
                                                            <Home size={16} /> 숙소 관리
                                                        </button>
                                                        <button
                                                            type="button"
                                                            onClick={() => {
                                                                setIsDropdownOpen(false);
                                                                navigate('/host/reviews');
                                                            }}
                                                            style={dropdownItemStyle}
                                                        >
                                                            <MessageSquare size={16} /> 리뷰 관리
                                                        </button>
                                                    </>
                                                ) : (
                                                    <button
                                                        type="button"
                                                        onClick={() => {
                                                            setIsDropdownOpen(false);
                                                            navigate('/host/accommodation');
                                                        }}
                                                        style={{
                                                            ...dropdownItemStyle,
                                                            color: '#0d9488',
                                                            fontWeight: 600
                                                        }}
                                                    >
                                                        <PlusCircle size={16} /> 숙소 신청 / 관리
                                                    </button>
                                                )}
                                            </>
                                        )}

                                        {userRole === 'ROLE_ADMIN' && (
                                            <>
                                                <button
                                                    type="button"
                                                    onClick={() => {
                                                        setIsDropdownOpen(false);
                                                        navigate('/admin/partners'); // 💡 파트너 관리 페이지로 이동!
                                                    }}
                                                    style={dropdownItemStyle}
                                                >
                                                    <Users size={16} /> 파트너 관리
                                                </button>
                                                <button
                                                    type="button"
                                                    onClick={() => {
                                                        setIsDropdownOpen(false);
                                                        navigate('/admin/accommodations');
                                                    }}
                                                    style={{
                                                        ...dropdownItemStyle,
                                                        color: '#c5221f',
                                                        fontWeight: 600
                                                    }}
                                                >
                                                    <FileCheck size={16} /> 신규 숙소 심사
                                                </button>
                                            </>
                                        )}

                                        <div style={{ height: '1px', backgroundColor: '#f0f0f0', margin: '6px 0' }} />
                                        <button
                                            type="button"
                                            onClick={() => {
                                                setIsDropdownOpen(false);
                                                navigate('/profile');
                                            }}
                                            style={dropdownItemStyle}
                                        >
                                            <Settings size={16} /> 회원정보 수정
                                        </button>
                                        <button
                                            type="button"
                                            onClick={handleLogoutClick}
                                            style={{ ...dropdownItemStyle, color: '#e53e3e' }}
                                        >
                                            <LogOut size={16} /> 로그아웃
                                        </button>
                                    </div>
                                )}
                            </div>

                            <NotificationBell userRole={userRole} isLoggedIn={isLoggedIn} />
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                            <Link
                                to="/login"
                                style={{
                                    fontSize: '14px',
                                    fontWeight: 600,
                                    color: '#123f4d',
                                    textDecoration: 'none',
                                    padding: '7px 14px',
                                    borderRadius: '6px',
                                    backgroundColor: '#f5f5f5',
                                }}
                            >
                                로그인
                            </Link>
                            <Link
                                to="/signup"
                                style={{
                                    fontSize: '14px',
                                    fontWeight: 600,
                                    color: '#ffffff',
                                    backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                    textDecoration: 'none',
                                    padding: '7px 14px',
                                    borderRadius: '6px',
                                }}
                            >
                                회원가입
                            </Link>
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
            </div>
        </header>
    );
}

const dropdownItemStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    display: 'flex',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: '10px',
    width: '100%',
    padding: '10px 16px',
    backgroundColor: 'transparent',
    border: 'none',
    textAlign: 'left',
    fontSize: '13px',
    fontWeight: 500,
    color: '#333333',
    cursor: 'pointer',
    transition: 'background-color 0.15s ease',
};

// File: src/components/home/StaySearchBar.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Calendar, Users, Search, ArrowRight, Map as MapIcon } from 'lucide-react';

interface StaySearchBarProps {
    todayStr: string;
    checkIn: string;
    checkOut: string;
    guests: number;
    query: string;
    onCheckInChange: (val: string) => void;
    onCheckOutChange: (val: string) => void;
    onGuestsChange: (val: number) => void;
    onQueryChange: (val: string) => void;
    onSearchSubmit: () => void;
    onMapSearch: () => void;
}

export function StaySearchBar({
                                  todayStr,
                                  checkIn,
                                  checkOut,
                                  guests,
                                  query,
                                  onCheckInChange,
                                  onCheckOutChange,
                                  onGuestsChange,
                                  onQueryChange,
                                  onSearchSubmit,
                                  onMapSearch
                              }: StaySearchBarProps) {
    const [isSearchPinned, setIsSearchPinned] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        const handleScroll = () => {
            setIsSearchPinned(window.scrollY > 430);
        };
        window.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll, { passive: true });
        return () => window.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
    }, []);

    return (
        <section
            className={`search-wrap container ${isSearchPinned ? 'is-pinned' : ''}`}
            aria-label="숙소 검색"
        >
            <form
                className="search-panel"
                onSubmit={(e) => {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    onSearchSubmit();
                }}
                style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '0' }}
            >
                {/* 1. 왼쪽 타이틀 영역 */}
                <div
                    className="search-heading"
                    style={{ minWidth: '165px', maxWidth: '175px', paddingRight: '14px', flexShrink: 0 }}
                >
                    <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>나에게 맞는 제주를 찾아보세요</span>
                    <strong style={{ fontSize: '17px', letterSpacing: '-0.5px', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                        어디로 떠나실까요?
                    </strong>
                </div>

                {/* 2. 일정 선택 필드 */}
                <div
                    className="search-field"
                    style={{
                        flex: '1.4',
                        minWidth: '260px',
                        padding: '8px 16px',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: '10px'
                    }}
                >
                    <Calendar size={20} color="#49717a" style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', width: '100%', minWidth: 0 }}>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#819296', fontWeight: 600 }}>일정</span>
                        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px', marginTop: '3px' }}>
                            <input
                                type="date"
                                min={todayStr}
                                value={checkIn}
                                onClick={(e) => (e.currentTarget as any).showPicker?.()}
                                onChange={(e) => {
                                    const nextIn = e.target.value;
                                    onCheckInChange(nextIn);
                                    if (nextIn >= checkOut) {
                                        const nextD = new Date(nextIn);
                                        nextD.setDate(nextD.getDate() + 1);
                                        onCheckOutChange(nextD.toISOString().split('T')[0]);
                                    }
                                }}
                                style={{
                                    border: 'none',
                                    outline: 'none',
                                    background: 'transparent',
                                    fontSize: '13px',
                                    fontWeight: 700,
                                    color: '#17343d',
                                    padding: 0,
                                    cursor: 'pointer',
                                    flex: 1,
                                    minWidth: '105px'
                                }}
                            />
                            <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '12px', flexShrink: 0 }}>~</span>
                            <input
                                type="date"
                                min={checkIn}
                                value={checkOut}
                                onClick={(e) => (e.currentTarget as any).showPicker?.()}
                                onChange={(e) => onCheckOutChange(e.target.value)}
                                style={{
                                    border: 'none',
                                    outline: 'none',
                                    background: 'transparent',
                                    fontSize: '13px',
                                    fontWeight: 700,
                                    color: '#17343d',
                                    padding: 0,
                                    cursor: 'pointer',
                                    flex: 1,
                                    minWidth: '105px'
                                }}
                            />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 3. 인원 선택 필드 */}
                <label className="search-field" style={{ flex: '0.8', minWidth: '130px', padding: '8px 16px' }}>
                    <Users size={20} color="#49717a" style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />
                    <span style={{ width: '100%' }}>
                        인원
                        <select
                            value={guests}
                            onChange={(e) => onGuestsChange(Number(e.target.value))}
                            style={{
                                display: 'block',
                                border: 'none',
                                outline: 'none',
                                background: 'transparent',
                                fontSize: '14px',
                                fontWeight: 700,
                                color: '#17343d',
                                cursor: 'pointer',
                                padding: '4px 0 0',
                                width: '100%'
                            }}
                        >
                            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8].map((num) => (
                                <option key={num} value={num}>성인 {num}명</option>
                            ))}
                        </select>
                    </span>
                </label>

                {/* 4. 검색어 필드 */}
                <label className="search-field keyword" style={{ flex: '1', minWidth: '150px', padding: '8px 16px' }}>
                    <Search size={20} color="#49717a" style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />
                    <span style={{ width: '100%' }}>
                        검색어
                        <input
                            value={query}
                            onChange={(e) => onQueryChange(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="숙소 또는 여행지"
                            style={{ width: '100%', fontSize: '14px', fontWeight: 600 }}
                        />
                    </span>
                </label>

                {/* 5. 검색 버튼 그룹 */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', paddingLeft: '12px', flexShrink: 0 }}>
                    <button
                        className="search-button"
                        type="submit"
                        style={{ padding: '16px 20px', fontSize: '14px', gap: '10px' }}
                    >
                        숙소 검색 <ArrowRight size={16} />
                    </button>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        className="search-button"
                        onClick={onMapSearch}
                        style={{
                            backgroundColor: '#0f766e',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            gap: '6px',
                            whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            padding: '16px 18px',
                            fontSize: '14px'
                        }}
                    >
                        <MapIcon size={17} />
                        지도 검색
                    </button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </section>
    );
}

// File: src/components/stay/StayMapModal.tsx

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Stay } from '../../types/stay.ts';
import { KakaoStayMap } from './KakaoMap.tsx';

interface StayMapModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    stays: Stay[];
}

export function StayMapModal({ isOpen, onClose, stays }: StayMapModalProps) {
    // 1. 모달 활성화 시 배경 스크롤 방지
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isOpen) {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        } else {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        }
        return () => {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        };
    }, [isOpen]);

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        <div
            style={{
                position: 'fixed',
                top: 0,
                left: 0,
                width: '100vw',
                height: '100vh',
                backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65)',
                backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
                zIndex: 3000,
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                padding: '24px',
            }}
            onClick={onClose}
        >
            <div
                style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    maxWidth: '1100px',
                    height: '85vh',
                    maxHeight: '780px',
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '24px',
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    display: 'flex',
                    flexDirection: 'column',
                    boxShadow: '0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35)',
                    padding: '28px',
                    position: 'relative',
                    boxSizing: 'border-box',
                }}

            >
                {/* 닫기 (X) 버튼 */}
                <button

                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        top: '24px',
                        right: '24px',
                        background: '#f1f5f9',
                        border: 'none',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        color: '#64748b',
                        width: '36px',
                        height: '36px',
                        borderRadius: '50%',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        transition: 'background-color 0.2s',
                        zIndex: 10,
                    }}
                    onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#e2e8f0')}
                    onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#f1f5f9')}
                >
                    <X size={20} />
                </button>

                {/* 2. 기존 홈페이지 section-top 헤더 영역 */}
                <div
                    style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        alignItems: 'flex-end',
                        marginBottom: '18px',
                        paddingRight: '48px', // X 버튼과 겹치지 않도록 여백
                    }}
                >
                    <div>
                        <span
                            className="eyebrow"
                            style={{
                                color: '#b45309',
                                fontSize: '12px',
                                fontWeight: 700,
                                letterSpacing: '0.05em',
                                display: 'block',
                                marginBottom: '4px',
                            }}
                        >
                            JEJU MAP GUIDE
                        </span>
                        <h2 style={{ fontSize: '24px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 6px 0' }}>
                            제주, 어디로 갈까요?
                        </h2>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', margin: 0 }}>
                            마커나 클러스터를 클릭하면 숙소 정보와 1박 요금을 확인하고 바로 이동할 수 있습니다.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <span
                        style={{
                            color: '#0f766e',
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            backgroundColor: '#ccfbf1',

                    </span>

                {/* 3. 지도 래퍼 카드 (모달 남은 높이를 꽉 채우도록 설정) */}
                <div
                    style={{
                        flex: 1,
                        minHeight: 0,
