// ==========================================
// File: src/components/common/WeatherWidget.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
    Cloud,
    Sun,
    CloudSun,
    CloudRain,
    CloudSnow,
    CloudLightning,
    CloudDrizzle,
    CloudFog
} from 'lucide-react';

interface WeatherData {
    temp: number;
    description: string;
    main: string;
    iconCode: string;
}

export function WeatherWidget() {
    const [weather, setWeather] = useState<WeatherData | null>(null);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

    useEffect(() => {
        let isMounted = true;

        const fetchWeather = async () => {
            try {
                // 💡 프론트엔드는 API 키나 외부 URL을 알 필요 없이 백엔드만 호출합니다.
                const weatherRes = await fetch('/api/v1/accommodations/weather');

                if (!weatherRes.ok) {
                    throw new Error('날씨 API 호출 실패');
                }

                const data = await weatherRes.json();

                if (isMounted) {
                    setWeather({
                        temp: Math.round(data.main.temp),
                        description: data.weather[0]?.description || '',
                        main: data.weather[0]?.main || 'Clouds',
                        iconCode: data.weather[0]?.icon || '01d',
                    });
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('날씨 정보를 불러오지 못했습니다:', err);
            } finally {
                if (isMounted) setLoading(false);
            }
        };

        fetchWeather();

        return () => {
            isMounted = false;
        };
    }, []);

    if (loading || !weather) {
        return null;
    }

    const renderWeatherIcon = () => {
        const main = weather.main.toLowerCase();
        const iconCode = weather.iconCode;
        const iconSize = 19;

        if (main.includes('clear')) {
            return <Sun size={iconSize} color="#f59e0b" fill="#fef3c7" strokeWidth={2} />;
        }
        if (main.includes('cloud')) {
            if (iconCode.startsWith('02')) {
                return <CloudSun size={iconSize} color="#475569" fill="#e2e8f0" strokeWidth={2} />;
            }
            return <Cloud size={iconSize} color="#64748b" fill="#cbd5e1" strokeWidth={2} />;
        }
        if (main.includes('rain')) {
            return <CloudRain size={iconSize} color="#0284c7" fill="#e0f2fe" strokeWidth={2} />;
        }
        if (main.includes('drizzle')) {
            return <CloudDrizzle size={iconSize} color="#38bdf8" strokeWidth={2} />;
        }
        if (main.includes('snow')) {
            return <CloudSnow size={iconSize} color="#67e8f9" strokeWidth={2} />;
        }
        if (main.includes('thunderstorm')) {
            return <CloudLightning size={iconSize} color="#eab308" strokeWidth={2} />;
        }
        return <CloudFog size={iconSize} color="#94a3b8" strokeWidth={2} />;
    };

    return (
        <div
            title={`제주도 현재 날씨: ${weather.description}`}
            style={{
                display: 'inline-flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '6px',
                padding: '4px 12px',
                backgroundColor: 'rgba(18, 63, 77, 0.05)',
                borderRadius: '24px',
                border: '1px solid #d5e0df',
                fontSize: '13px',
                fontWeight: 700,
                color: '#17343d',
                userSelect: 'none',
                cursor: 'default',
                height: '34px',
                boxSizing: 'border-box',
            }}
        >
            <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 800 }}>제주</span>
            <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
                {renderWeatherIcon()}
            </span>
            <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', letterSpacing: '-0.3px' }}>{weather.temp}°C</span>
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/components/common/LocationMapPopupModal.tsx
// ==========================================
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';

interface LocationMapPopupModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    eyebrow?: string;
    title: string;
    children: React.ReactNode;
}

export function LocationMapPopupModal({
                                          isOpen,
                                          onClose,
                                          eyebrow = 'LOCATION MAP',
                                          title,
                                          children
                                      }: LocationMapPopupModalProps) {
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isOpen) {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        } else {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        }
        return () => {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        };
    }, [isOpen]);

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        <div
            style={{
                position: 'fixed',
                top: 0,
                left: 0,
                width: '100vw',
                height: '100vh',
                backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65)',
                backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
                zIndex: 3000,
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                padding: '24px'
            }}
            onClick={onClose}
        >
            <div
                style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    maxWidth: '1000px',
                    height: '82vh',
                    maxHeight: '740px',
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '24px',
                    padding: '24px 28px',
                    display: 'flex',
                    flexDirection: 'column',
                    boxShadow: '0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35)',
                    position: 'relative',
                    boxSizing: 'border-box'
                }}
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '16px' }}>
                    <div>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f766e', display: 'block', marginBottom: '2px' }}>
                            {eyebrow}
                        </span>
                        <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '20px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a' }}>
                            {title} 위치 안내
                        </h3>
                        <p style={{ margin: '4px 0 0 0', fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b' }}>
                            마우스 휠 또는 우측 하단 +/- 버튼으로 지도를 자유롭게 확대·축소할 수 있습니다.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={onClose}
                        style={{
                            width: '36px',
                            height: '36px',
                            borderRadius: '50%',
                            border: 'none',
                            backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
                            color: '#64748b',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            cursor: 'pointer'
                        }}
                    >
                        <X size={20} />
                    </button>
                </div>

                <div style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0, borderRadius: '16px', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
                    {children}
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/components/tour/KakaoTourMap.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Plus, Minus } from 'lucide-react';
import type { TourSummary } from '../../api/tours.ts';

declare const window: typeof globalThis & {
    kakao: any;
};

interface KakaoTourMapProps {
    tours: TourSummary[];
    level?: number;
    height?: string;
    mode?: 'search' | 'detail';
    zoomable?: boolean;
    onNavigateDetail?: () => void;
    storageKey?: string; // 💡 뒤로가기 시 마지막 지도 좌표/줌 레벨 복원용 키
}

export function KakaoTourMap({
                                 tours,
                                 level,
                                 height = '520px',
                                 mode = 'search',
                                 zoomable = true,
                                 onNavigateDetail,
                                 storageKey = 'jeju_tour_map_view'
                             }: KakaoTourMapProps) {
    const mapContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const mapInstanceRef = useRef<any>(null);
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [isLoaded, setIsLoaded] = useState(false);

    const onNavigateDetailRef = useRef(onNavigateDetail);
    const navigateRef = useRef(navigate);

    useEffect(() => {
        onNavigateDetailRef.current = onNavigateDetail;
        navigateRef.current = navigate;
    }, [onNavigateDetail, navigate]);

    // 1. 카카오 지도 SDK 동적 로드
    useEffect(() => {
        let isMounted = true;

        const initKakaoMap = async () => {
            const existingScript = document.getElementById('kakao-map-sdk');
            if (existingScript) {
                if (window.kakao && window.kakao.maps) {
                    if (isMounted) setIsLoaded(true);
                } else {
                    existingScript.addEventListener('load', () => {
                        if (isMounted) setIsLoaded(true);
                    });
                }
                return;
            }

            let appKey = import.meta.env.VITE_KAKAO_MAP_KEY || '';
            if (!appKey) {
                try {
                    const res = await fetch('/api/v1/accommodations/map-key');
                    if (res.ok) {
                        const data = await res.json();
                        appKey = data.mapKey;
                    }
                } catch (e) {
                    console.error('카카오 지도 키를 가져오지 못했습니다.', e);
                }
            }

            if (!appKey || !isMounted) return;

            const script = document.createElement('script');
            script.id = 'kakao-map-sdk';
            script.src = `//dapi.kakao.com/v2/maps/sdk.js?appkey=${appKey}&libraries=clusterer,services&autoload=false`;
            script.async = true;
            script.onload = () => {
                if (isMounted) setIsLoaded(true);
            };
            document.head.appendChild(script);
        };

        initKakaoMap();
        return () => {
            isMounted = false;
        };
    }, []);

    // 2. 마커 및 커스텀 오버레이 렌더링
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isLoaded || !window.kakao || !window.kakao.maps) return;

        window.kakao.maps.load(() => {
            if (!mapContainerRef.current) return;

            const validTours = tours.filter(
                (t) => t.mapX && t.mapY && !isNaN(Number(t.mapX)) && !isNaN(Number(t.mapY))
            );

            const isDetailPage = mode === 'detail';
            const isSingleTour = validTours.length === 1;

            // 💡 검색 모드일 때 이전에 저장된 지도 중심/레벨이 있으면 복원
            let savedView: { lat: number; lng: number; level: number } | null = null;
            if (!isDetailPage && storageKey) {
                try {
                    const raw = sessionStorage.getItem(storageKey);
                    if (raw) savedView = JSON.parse(raw);
                } catch {}
            }

            const centerPosition = (isDetailPage || isSingleTour) && validTours.length > 0
                ? new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(
                    parseFloat(validTours[0].mapY!),
                    parseFloat(validTours[0].mapX!)
                )
                : savedView
                    ? new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(savedView.lat, savedView.lng)
                    : new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(33.3617, 126.5292);

            const zoomLevel = (isDetailPage || isSingleTour)
                ? (level ?? (isDetailPage ? 4 : 6))
                : (savedView?.level ?? level ?? 9);

            const map = new window.kakao.maps.Map(mapContainerRef.current, {
                center: centerPosition,
                level: zoomLevel,
            });
            mapInstanceRef.current = map;

            map.setZoomable(zoomable);

            // 지도 이동/확대/축소 시 현재 뷰 상태 저장 (뒤로가기 복원용)
            if (!isDetailPage && storageKey) {
                const saveMapState = () => {
                    const c = map.getCenter();
                    sessionStorage.setItem(
                        storageKey,
                        JSON.stringify({ lat: c.getLat(), lng: c.getLng(), level: map.getLevel() })
                    );
                };
                window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(map, 'idle', saveMapState);
            }

            // [케이스 A] 관광지 상세페이지 하단 지도 모드
            if (isDetailPage && validTours.length > 0) {
                const tour = validTours[0];
                const marker = new window.kakao.maps.Marker({
                    position: centerPosition,
                    map: map,
                });

                const fullAddr = `${tour.addr1 || ''} ${tour.addr2 || ''}`.trim();
                const uid = `${tour.contentId}-${zoomable ? 'popup' : 'inline'}`;

                const detailCard = document.createElement('div');
                detailCard.style.cssText = `
                    background: #ffffff;
                    border-radius: 12px;
                    box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.22);
                    border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                    padding: 12px 14px;
                    min-width: 220px;
                    max-width: 280px;
                    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
                    position: relative;
                    z-index: 9999;
                `;

                detailCard.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-start; margin-bottom: 2px;">
                        <span style="font-size: 11px; color: #0f766e; font-weight: 700;">JEJU ATTRACTION</span>
                        <button id="btn-close-tour-detail-overlay-${uid}" style="
                            background: none; border: none; font-size: 14px; color: #94a3b8; cursor: pointer; padding: 0 0 0 8px; line-height: 1;
                        ">✕</button>
                    </div>
                    <strong style="display: block; font-size: 14px; color: #0f172a; margin-bottom: 4px; line-height: 1.3;">
                        ${tour.title}
                    </strong>
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #64748b; line-height: 1.4; margin-bottom: 8px; word-break: keep-all;">
                        ${fullAddr || '등록된 주소 정보 없음'}
                    </div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px;">
                        <button id="btn-copy-tour-addr-${uid}" style="
                            flex: 1; padding: 6px 0; background-color: #f1f5f9; color: #123f4d;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer;
                        ">주소 복사</button>
                        <button id="btn-close-tour-bottom-${uid}" style="
                            padding: 6px 12px; background-color: #ffffff; color: #64748b;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 11px; cursor: pointer;
                        ">닫기</button>
                    </div>
                `;

                const overlay = new window.kakao.maps.CustomOverlay({
                    content: detailCard,
                    position: centerPosition,
                    yAnchor: 1.3,
                    zIndex: 9999, // 💡 다른 마커/핀에 절대 가려지지 않도록 최상단 고정
                });

                overlay.setMap(map);

                window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(marker, 'click', () => {
                    overlay.setMap(map);
                });

                setTimeout(() => {
                    const copyBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-copy-tour-addr-${uid}`);
                    const closeXBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-close-tour-detail-overlay-${uid}`);
                    const closeBottomBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-close-tour-bottom-${uid}`);

                    if (copyBtn && fullAddr) {
                        copyBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            navigator.clipboard.writeText(fullAddr);
                            alert('주소가 클립보드에 복사되었습니다.');
                        };
                    }
                    if (closeXBtn) {
                        closeXBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            overlay.setMap(null);
                        };
                    }
                    if (closeBottomBtn) {
                        closeBottomBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            overlay.setMap(null);
                        };
                    }
                }, 0);

                return;
            }

            // [케이스 B] 관광 지도 모달 모드 (전체 관광지 탐색)
            const clusterer = new window.kakao.maps.MarkerClusterer({
                map: map,
                averageCenter: true,
                minLevel: 6,
                disableClickZoom: false,
            });

            let currentOverlay: any = null;
            let currentActiveMarker: any = null;

            const closeCurrentOverlay = () => {
                if (currentOverlay) {
                    currentOverlay.setMap(null);
                    currentOverlay = null;
                }
                if (currentActiveMarker) {
                    currentActiveMarker.setZIndex(1);
                    currentActiveMarker = null;
                }
            };

            // 💡 지도 빈 공간을 클릭하거나 줌 레벨이 변경되어 클러스터가 다시 묶일 때 이전 오버레이가 다른 핀과 겹치지 않게 정리
            window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(map, 'click', closeCurrentOverlay);
            window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(map, 'zoom_start', closeCurrentOverlay);

            const markers = validTours.map((tour) => {
                const lat = parseFloat(tour.mapY!);
                const lng = parseFloat(tour.mapX!);
                const position = new window.kakao.maps.LatLng(lat, lng);

                const marker = new window.kakao.maps.Marker({
                    position,
                    zIndex: 1
                });

                const fullAddr = `${tour.addr1 || ''} ${tour.addr2 || ''}`.trim();
                const thumbHtml = tour.firstImage
                    ? `<div style="width: 100%; height: 100px; border-radius: 8px; background-image: url('${tour.firstImage}'); background-size: cover; background-position: center; margin-bottom: 8px; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;"></div>`
                    : '';

                const content = document.createElement('div');
                content.style.cssText = `
                    background: #ffffff;
                    border-radius: 14px;
                    box-shadow: 0 10px 28px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25);
                    border: 1.5px solid #0f766e;
                    padding: 12px 14px;
                    width: 240px;
                    position: relative;
                    z-index: 9999;
                    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
                `;

                // 오버레이 내부 클릭/휠 이벤트가 지도로 전파되어 가려지는 현상 방지
                content.onmousedown = (e) => e.stopPropagation();
                content.onclick = (e) => e.stopPropagation();

                content.innerHTML = `
                    ${thumbHtml}
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #0f766e; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 2px;">
                        제주 관광명소
                    </div>
                    <strong style="display: block; font-size: 14px; color: #0f172a; margin-bottom: 4px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;">
                        ${tour.title}
                    </strong>
                    <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #64748b; line-height: 1.3; margin-bottom: 10px; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;">
                        ${fullAddr || '제주특별자치도'}
                    </div>
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px;">
                        <button id="btn-tour-detail-${tour.contentId}" style="
                            flex: 1; padding: 7px 0; background-color: #123f4d; color: #ffffff;
                            border: none; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            상세보기
                        </button>
                        <button id="btn-tour-close-${tour.contentId}" style="
                            padding: 7px 10px; background-color: #f1f5f9; color: #475569;
                            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; border-radius: 6px; font-size: 12px; cursor: pointer;
                        ">
                            닫기
                        </button>
                    </div>
                `;

                const overlay = new window.kakao.maps.CustomOverlay({
                    content: content,
                    position: position,
                    yAnchor: 1.15,
                    zIndex: 9999, // 💡 다른 마커/클러스터 핀보다 무조건 앞에 띄움!
                    clickable: true,
                });

                const showOverlay = () => {
                    closeCurrentOverlay();
                    marker.setZIndex(100);
                    overlay.setZIndex(9999);
                    overlay.setMap(map);
                    currentOverlay = overlay;
                    currentActiveMarker = marker;

                    setTimeout(() => {
                        const detailBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-tour-detail-${tour.contentId}`);
                        const closeBtn = document.getElementById(`btn-tour-close-${tour.contentId}`);

                        if (detailBtn) {
                            detailBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                                e.stopPropagation();
                                if (onNavigateDetailRef.current) onNavigateDetailRef.current();
                                navigateRef.current(`/tours/${tour.contentId}`);
                            };
                        }
                        if (closeBtn) {
                            closeBtn.onclick = (e) => {
                                e.stopPropagation();
                                closeCurrentOverlay();
                            };
                        }
                    }, 0);
                };

                window.kakao.maps.event.addListener(marker, 'click', showOverlay);

                if (isSingleTour) showOverlay();
                return marker;
            });

            if (isSingleTour && markers.length > 0) {
                markers[0].setMap(map);
            } else if (markers.length > 0) {
                clusterer.addMarkers(markers);
            }
        });
    }, [isLoaded, tours, level, mode, zoomable, storageKey]);

    const handleZoomIn = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (mapInstanceRef.current) {
            mapInstanceRef.current.setLevel(mapInstanceRef.current.getLevel() - 1, { animate: true });
        }
    };

    const handleZoomOut = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (mapInstanceRef.current) {
            mapInstanceRef.current.setLevel(mapInstanceRef.current.getLevel() + 1, { animate: true });
        }
    };

    return (
        <div style={{ position: 'relative', width: '100%', height }}>
            <div
                ref={mapContainerRef}
                style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    height: '100%',
                    borderRadius: '16px',
                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'center',
                }}
            >
                {!isLoaded && (
                    <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '14px' }}>
                        관광 지도를 불러오는 중입니다...
                    </span>
                )}
            </div>

            {isLoaded && (
                <div
                    onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        right: '14px',
                        bottom: '14px',
                        zIndex: 10,
                        display: 'flex',
                        flexDirection: 'column',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '10px',
                        boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                        border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                        overflow: 'hidden',
                    }}
                >
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={handleZoomIn}
                        title="지도 확대"
                        aria-label="지도 확대"
                        style={{
                            width: '36px',
                            height: '36px',
                            border: 'none',
                            borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                            color: '#123f4d',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                        }}
                    >
                        <Plus size={18} strokeWidth={2.5} />
                    </button>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={handleZoomOut}
                        title="지도 축소"
                        aria-label="지도 축소"
                        style={{
                            width: '36px',
                            height: '36px',
                            border: 'none',
                            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                            color: '#123f4d',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                        }}
                    >
                        <Minus size={18} strokeWidth={2.5} />
                    </button>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/components/tour/TourWishlistCard.tsx
// ==========================================
import React from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Heart, MapPin, Clock, Phone, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';
import type { TourSummary } from '../../api/tours.ts'

function cleanHtmlText(text?: string): string {
    if (!text) return '';
    return text
        .replace(/<br\s*\/?>/gi, ' ')
        .replace(/&nbsp;/gi, ' ')
        .replace(/<[^>]*>?/gm, '')
        .trim();
}

interface TourWishlistCardProps {
    tour: TourSummary;
    isRemoving: boolean;
    onRemoveLike: (e: React.MouseEvent, contentId: string) => void;
}

export function TourWishlistCard({
                                     tour,
                                     isRemoving,
                                     onRemoveLike
                                 }: TourWishlistCardProps) {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const fullAddr = `${tour.addr1 || ''} ${tour.addr2 || ''}`.trim();
    const summaryText = cleanHtmlText(tour.overview);

    return (
        <article
            className="stay-card"
            onClick={() => navigate(`/tours/${tour.contentId}`)}
            style={{
                cursor: 'pointer',
                position: 'relative',
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'column',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                opacity: isRemoving ? 0 : 1,
                transform: isRemoving ? 'scale(0.92)' : 'scale(1)',
                pointerEvents: isRemoving ? 'none' : 'auto',
                transition: 'opacity 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1), transform 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)',
            }}
        >
            <div>
                {/* 상단 썸네일 이미지 및 찜 해제 하트 버튼 */}
                <div
                    className="stay-photo"
                    style={{
                        backgroundImage: `url(${tour.firstImage || '/jeju-stays.png'})`,
                        backgroundSize: 'cover',
                        backgroundPosition: 'center',
                        backgroundRepeat: 'no-repeat',
                        position: 'relative',
                    }}
                >
                    <span className="photo-tag">제주 관광명소</span>

                    <div
                        onClick={(e) => onRemoveLike(e, tour.contentId)}
                        role="button"
                        aria-label="관광지 위시리스트 해제"
                        style={{
                            position: 'absolute',
                            top: '12px',
                            right: '12px',
                            width: '36px',
                            height: '36px',
                            borderRadius: '50%',
                            backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9)',
                            backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                            zIndex: 2,
                            transition: 'transform 0.15s ease',
                        }}
                        onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.1)')}
                        onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.0)')}
                    >
                        <Heart
                            size={19}
                            fill="#ef4444"
                            color="#ef4444"
                            strokeWidth={2}
                        />
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 하단 관광지 정보 영역 */}
                <div className="stay-info">
                    <div className="stay-location" style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                        <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px' }}>
                            <MapPin size={14} />
                            {fullAddr.includes('서귀포') ? '서귀포시' : '제주시'}
                        </span>
                    </div>

                    <h3>{tour.title}</h3>

                    <p style={{ marginBottom: '10px' }}>
                        {summaryText
                            ? `${summaryText.slice(0, 75)}${summaryText.length > 75 ? '...' : ''}`
                            : fullAddr || '제주의 아름다운 대표 관광명소입니다.'}
                    </p>

                    {/* 이용시간 / 연락처 요약 */}
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '4px', fontSize: '12px', color: '#64748b', marginBottom: '12px' }}>
                        {tour.useTime && (
                            <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '5px', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis' }}>
                                <Clock size={13} color="#0f766e" style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />
                                {cleanHtmlText(tour.useTime)}
                            </span>
                        )}
                        {tour.tel && (
                            <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '5px' }}>
                                <Phone size={13} color="#0f766e" style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />
                                {cleanHtmlText(tour.tel)}
                            </span>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="stay-bottom">
                        <div>
                            <strong style={{ fontSize: '14px', color: '#0f766e' }}>
                                {tour.useFee ? cleanHtmlText(tour.useFee).slice(0, 18) : '상세 안내 확인'}
                            </strong>
                        </div>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={(e) => {
                                e.stopPropagation();
                                navigate(`/tours/${tour.contentId}`);
                            }}
                            aria-label={`${tour.title} 상세보기`}
                        >
                            <ArrowRight size={19} />
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </article>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/components/tour/TourSliderSection.tsx
// ==========================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
    ChevronLeft,
    ChevronRight,
    Heart,
    Navigation,
    MapPin
} from 'lucide-react';
import { fetchMyTourWishlist, toggleTourLike } from '../../api/tours.ts';

export interface TourSliderItem {
    contentId: string;
    title: string;
    firstImage?: string;
    addr1?: string;
    distanceKm?: number; // 💡 숙소 상세페이지에서는 전달되고, 홈페이지에서는 생략됨
}

interface TourSliderSectionProps {
    tours: TourSliderItem[];
    headerRender?: React.ReactNode; // 상단 좌측 제목 영역 커스텀
    footerRender?: React.ReactNode; // 하단 '더 많은 관광지를 둘러보세요' 버튼 등 커스텀
    containerStyle?: React.CSSProperties;
}

export function TourSliderSection({
                                      tours,
                                      headerRender,
                                      footerRender,
                                      containerStyle
                                  }: TourSliderSectionProps) {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [slideIndex, setSlideIndex] = useState(0);
    const [likedTourIds, setLikedTourIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());

    const VISIBLE_CARDS = 3;
    const CARD_GAP = 16;
    const displayTours = tours.slice(0, 9); // 최대 9개 노출
    const maxSlideIndex = Math.max(0, displayTours.length - VISIBLE_CARDS);

    const currentPage = Math.floor(slideIndex / VISIBLE_CARDS) + 1;
    const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(displayTours.length / VISIBLE_CARDS));

    // 💡 전달받은 관광지 목록(tours)이 바뀌면 슬라이더를 첫 페이지(0)로 리셋
    useEffect(() => {
        setSlideIndex(0);
    }, [tours]);

    // 로그인한 유저의 관광지 찜 목록 동기화
    useEffect(() => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            setLikedTourIds(new Set());
            return;
        }
        fetchMyTourWishlist()
            .then((list) => {
                setLikedTourIds(new Set(list.map((t) => t.contentId)));
            })
            .catch(() => setLikedTourIds(new Set()));
    }, []);

    // 찜(하트) 토글 핸들러
    const handleToggleLike = async (e: React.MouseEvent, contentId: string) => {
        e.stopPropagation();

        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('로그인이 필요한 기능입니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }

        try {
            const res = await toggleTourLike(contentId);
            setLikedTourIds((prev) => {
                const next = new Set(prev);
                if (res.liked) {
                    next.add(contentId);
                } else {
                    next.delete(contentId);
                }
                return next;
            });
        } catch {
            alert('관광지 위시리스트 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
        }
    };

    if (displayTours.length === 0) return null;

    return (
        <div style={containerStyle}>
            {/* 상단 헤더 + 우측 3개 단위 페이지 화살표 컨트롤 */}
            <div style={{
                display: 'flex',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                alignItems: 'flex-end',
                marginBottom: '22px',
                flexWrap: 'wrap',
                gap: '12px'
            }}>
                <div>{headerRender}</div>

                {displayTours.length > VISIBLE_CARDS && (
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#64748b', fontWeight: 700, marginRight: '4px' }}>
                            {currentPage} / {totalPages} 페이지
                        </span>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            disabled={slideIndex === 0}
                            onClick={() => setSlideIndex((prev) => Math.max(0, prev - VISIBLE_CARDS))}
                            aria-label="이전 3개 관광지 보기"
                            style={{
                                width: '38px',
                                height: '38px',
                                borderRadius: '50%',
                                border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                backgroundColor: slideIndex === 0 ? '#f8fafc' : '#ffffff',
                                color: slideIndex === 0 ? '#cbd5e1' : '#123f4d',
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                justifyContent: 'center',
                                cursor: slideIndex === 0 ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                                transition: 'all 0.2s ease',
                                boxShadow: slideIndex === 0 ? 'none' : '0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.06)'
                            }}
                        >
                            <ChevronLeft size={18} />
                        </button>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            disabled={slideIndex >= maxSlideIndex}
                            onClick={() => setSlideIndex((prev) => Math.min(maxSlideIndex, prev + VISIBLE_CARDS))}
                            aria-label="다음 3개 관광지 보기"
                            style={{
                                width: '38px',
                                height: '38px',
                                borderRadius: '50%',
                                border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                backgroundColor: slideIndex >= maxSlideIndex ? '#f8fafc' : '#ffffff',
                                color: slideIndex >= maxSlideIndex ? '#cbd5e1' : '#123f4d',
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                justifyContent: 'center',
                                cursor: slideIndex >= maxSlideIndex ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                                transition: 'all 0.2s ease',
                                boxShadow: slideIndex >= maxSlideIndex ? 'none' : '0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.06)'
                            }}
                        >
                            <ChevronRight size={18} />
                        </button>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>

            {/* 3개 노출 슬라이더 뷰포트 */}
            <div style={{ overflow: 'hidden', width: '100%', padding: '4px 0' }}>
                <div
                    style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        gap: `${CARD_GAP}px`,
                        transform: `translateX(calc(-${slideIndex} * ((100% + ${CARD_GAP}px) / ${VISIBLE_CARDS})))`,
                        transition: 'transform 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.25, 1, 0.5, 1)',
                    }}
                >
                    {displayTours.map((tour) => {
                        const isLiked = likedTourIds.has(tour.contentId);
                        const hasDistance = tour.distanceKm !== undefined && tour.distanceKm !== null;
                        const regionLabel = tour.addr1?.includes('서귀포') ? '서귀포시' : '제주시';

                        return (
                            <article
                                key={tour.contentId}
                                onClick={() => navigate(`/tours/${tour.contentId}`)}
                                style={{
                                    flex: `0 0 calc((100% - ${(VISIBLE_CARDS - 1) * CARD_GAP}px) / ${VISIBLE_CARDS})`,
                                    borderRadius: '16px',
                                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                    overflow: 'hidden',
                                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                                    cursor: 'pointer',
                                    boxShadow: '0 2px 10px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04)',
                                    transition: 'transform 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease',
                                }}
                                onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                                    e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'translateY(-4px)';
                                    e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = '0 10px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.09)';
                                }}
                                onMouseLeave={(e) => {
                                    e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'translateY(0)';
                                    e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow = '0 2px 10px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04)';
                                }}
                            >
                                {/* 상단 썸네일 이미지 + 좌측 상단(거리 또는 지역) 뱃지 + 우측 상단 찜 하트 */}
                                <div
                                    style={{
                                        width: '100%',
                                        height: '185px',
                                        backgroundImage: `url(${tour.firstImage || '/jeju-stays.png'})`,
                                        backgroundSize: 'cover',
                                        backgroundPosition: 'center',
                                        position: 'relative',
                                        backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9'
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <span
                                        style={{
                                            position: 'absolute',
                                            top: '12px',
                                            left: '12px',
                                            backgroundColor: 'rgba(18, 63, 77, 0.88)',
                                            backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
                                            color: '#ffffff',
                                            padding: '4px 10px',
                                            borderRadius: '20px',
                                            fontSize: '11px',
                                            fontWeight: 700,
                                            display: 'inline-flex',
                                            alignItems: 'center',
                                            gap: '4px',
                                            boxShadow: '0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.15)'
                                        }}
                                    >
                                        {hasDistance ? (
                                            <>
                                                <Navigation size={11} color="#5eead4" />
                                                숙소에서 {tour.distanceKm}km
                                            </>
                                        ) : (
                                            <>
                                                <MapPin size={11} color="#5eead4" />
                                                {regionLabel}
                                            </>
                                        )}
                                    </span>

                                    {/* 우측 상단 관광지 찜(하트) 버튼 */}
                                    <div
                                        onClick={(e) => handleToggleLike(e, tour.contentId)}
                                        role="button"
                                        aria-label="관광지 위시리스트 토글"
                                        style={{
                                            position: 'absolute',
                                            top: '12px',
                                            right: '12px',
                                            width: '34px',
                                            height: '34px',
                                            borderRadius: '50%',
                                            backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9)',
                                            backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
                                            display: 'flex',
                                            alignItems: 'center',
                                            justifyContent: 'center',
                                            cursor: 'pointer',
                                            boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                                            zIndex: 2,
                                            transition: 'transform 0.15s ease',
                                        }}
                                        onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.12)')}
                                        onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.0)')}
                                    >
                                        <Heart
                                            size={18}
                                            fill={isLiked ? '#ef4444' : 'none'}
                                            color={isLiked ? '#ef4444' : '#64748b'}
                                            strokeWidth={isLiked ? 2 : 1.8}
                                        />
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                {/* 하단 텍스트 영역 */}
                                <div style={{ padding: '16px' }}>
                                    <span style={{
                                        fontSize: '11px',
                                        fontWeight: 700,
                                        color: '#0f766e',
                                        display: 'block',
                                        marginBottom: '4px'
                                    }}>
                                        제주 추천 명소
                                    </span>
                                    <h3 style={{
                                        fontSize: '16px',
                                        fontWeight: 700,
                                        color: '#0f172a',
                                        margin: '0 0 10px 0',
                                        whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                                        overflow: 'hidden',
                                        textOverflow: 'ellipsis'
                                    }}>
                                        {tour.title}
                                    </h3>
                                    <div style={{
                                        display: 'flex',
                                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                                        alignItems: 'center',
                                        paddingTop: '10px',
                                        borderTop: '1px solid #f1f5f9',
                                        fontSize: '12px',
                                        color: '#64748b',
                                        fontWeight: 600
                                    }}>
                                        <span>관광지 상세정보 보기</span>
                                        <ChevronRight size={15} color="#0f766e" />
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </article>
                        );
                    })}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* 하단 추가 버튼 영역 (홈페이지의 '더 많은 관광지를 둘러보세요' 버튼 등) */}
            {footerRender && (
                <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '32px' }}>
                    {footerRender}
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/components/tour/TourMapModal.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { X, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import type { TourSummary } from '../../api/tours.ts';
import { KakaoTourMap } from './KakaoTourMap.tsx';

interface TourMapModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    tours: TourSummary[];
    storageKeyPrefix?: string; // 💡 페이지별(홈페이지 vs 위시리스트) 검색어 분리 저장용
}

export function TourMapModal({
                                 isOpen,
                                 onClose,
                                 tours,
                                 storageKeyPrefix = 'home_tour_map'
                             }: TourMapModalProps) {
    const keywordStorageKey = `${storageKeyPrefix}_keyword`;
    const viewStorageKey = `${storageKeyPrefix}_view`;

    // 💡 sessionStorage에서 이전 검색어 복원 (상세페이지 갔다가 뒤로가기 해도 그대로 유지됨!)
    const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState<string>(() => {
        return sessionStorage.getItem(keywordStorageKey) || '';
    });

    // 검색어가 바뀔 때마다 sessionStorage에 실시간 저장
    useEffect(() => {
        sessionStorage.setItem(keywordStorageKey, keyword);
    }, [keyword, keywordStorageKey]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (isOpen) {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        } else {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        }
        return () => {
            document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
        };
    }, [isOpen]);

    // 💡 사용자가 직접 X 버튼이나 모달 바깥 배경을 눌러 닫을 때만 검색어와 지도 위치를 초기화
    const handleExplicitClose = () => {
        sessionStorage.removeItem(keywordStorageKey);
        sessionStorage.removeItem(viewStorageKey);
        setKeyword('');
        onClose();
    };

    const filteredTours = useMemo(() => {
        if (!keyword.trim()) return tours;
        const kw = keyword.trim().toLowerCase();
        return tours.filter(
            (t) =>
                t.title?.toLowerCase().includes(kw) ||
                t.addr1?.toLowerCase().includes(kw) ||
                t.addr2?.toLowerCase().includes(kw)
        );
    }, [tours, keyword]);

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    return (
        <div
            style={{
                position: 'fixed',
                top: 0,
                left: 0,
                width: '100vw',
                height: '100vh',
                backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.65)',
                backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
                zIndex: 3000,
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                padding: '24px',
            }}
            onClick={handleExplicitClose}
        >
            <div
                style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    maxWidth: '1100px',
                    height: '85vh',
                    maxHeight: '780px',
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '24px',
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    display: 'flex',
                    flexDirection: 'column',
                    boxShadow: '0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35)',
                    padding: '28px',
                    position: 'relative',
                    boxSizing: 'border-box',
                }}
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
                <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleExplicitClose}
                    aria-label="닫기"
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        top: '24px',
                        right: '24px',
                        background: '#f1f5f9',
                        border: 'none',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        color: '#64748b',
                        width: '36px',
                        height: '36px',
                        borderRadius: '50%',
                        display: 'flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        justifyContent: 'center',
                        zIndex: 10,
                    }}
                >
                    <X size={20} />
                </button>

                {/* 상단 헤더 및 검색바 */}
                <div
                    style={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        alignItems: 'flex-end',
                        flexWrap: 'wrap',
                        gap: '12px',
                        marginBottom: '18px',
                        paddingRight: '48px',
                    }}
                >
                    <div>
                        <span
                            style={{
                                color: '#0f766e',
                                fontSize: '12px',
                                fontWeight: 800,
                                letterSpacing: '0.05em',
                                display: 'block',
                                marginBottom: '4px',
                            }}
                        >
                            JEJU TOUR ATTRACTION MAP
                        </span>
                        <h2 style={{ fontSize: '24px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 6px 0' }}>
                            제주 관광 지도
                        </h2>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', margin: 0 }}>
                            지도 위의 마커를 클릭하면 관광지 사진과 정보를 확인하고 상세페이지로 이동할 수 있습니다.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' }}>
                        <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                value={keyword}
                                onChange={(e) => setKeyword(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="관광지명 또는 지역 검색"
                                style={{
                                    padding: '8px 14px 8px 34px',
                                    borderRadius: '20px',
                                    border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                    fontSize: '13px',
                                    outline: 'none',
                                    width: '210px',
                                }}
                            />
                            <Search size={15} color="#64748b" style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '12px', top: '10px' }} />
                        </div>

                        <span
                            style={{
                                color: '#0f766e',
                                fontWeight: 700,
                                backgroundColor: '#ccfbf1',
                                padding: '7px 14px',
                                borderRadius: '20px',
                                fontSize: '12px',
                                whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                            }}
                        >
                            관광명소 {filteredTours.length}곳 표시 중
                        </span>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 지도 영역 (💡 상세보기로 이동할 때 onClose를 호출하지 않으므로 뒤로가기 시 그대로 열려 있음!) */}
                <div
                    style={{
                        flex: 1,
                        minHeight: 0,
                        borderRadius: '18px',
                        overflow: 'hidden',
                        border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                        position: 'relative',
                    }}
                >
                    <KakaoTourMap
                        tours={filteredTours}
                        level={9}
                        height="100%"
                        mode="search"
                        storageKey={viewStorageKey}
                    />
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/components/admim/AdminRejectModal.tsx
// ==========================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';
import type { PendingAccommodationItem} from "../../pages/admin/AdminAccommodationsPage.tsx";

interface AdminRejectModalProps {
    target: PendingAccommodationItem | null;
    isProcessing: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    onConfirmReject: (contentId: string, rejectReason: string) => void;
}

export function AdminRejectModal({
                                     target,
                                     isProcessing,
                                     onClose,
                                     onConfirmReject
                                 }: AdminRejectModalProps) {
    const [rejectReason, setRejectReason] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        if (target) setRejectReason('');
    }, [target]);

    if (!target) return null;

    const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!rejectReason.trim()) {
            alert('반려 사유를 입력해 주세요.');
            return;
        }
        onConfirmReject(target.contentId, rejectReason.trim());
    };

    return (
        <div
            style={{
                position: 'fixed',
                top: 0,
                left: 0,
                width: '100vw',
                height: '100vh',
                backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6)',
                backdropFilter: 'blur(3px)',
                zIndex: 3000,
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                padding: '20px'
            }}
            onClick={onClose}
        >
            <div
                style={{
                    width: '100%',
                    maxWidth: '460px',
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '18px',
                    padding: '28px',
                    boxShadow: '0 20px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25)',
                    position: 'relative'
                }}
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
                <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={onClose}
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        top: '20px',
                        right: '20px',
                        background: 'none',
                        border: 'none',
                        cursor: 'pointer',
                        color: '#64748b'
                    }}
                >
                    <X size={20} />
                </button>

                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', marginBottom: '8px', color: '#e11d48' }}>
                    <AlertTriangle size={22} />
                    <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a' }}>
                        숙소 등록 반려 처리
                    </h3>
                </div>

                <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', margin: '0 0 18px 0', lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
                    <strong>[{target.title}]</strong> 숙소의 반려 사유를 작성해 주세요.<br />
                    입력하신 사유는 호스트의 알림창과 숙소 관리 페이지에 표시됩니다.
                </p>

                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
                    <textarea
                        rows={4}
                        required
                        value={rejectReason}
                        onChange={(e) => setRejectReason(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="예: 대표 이미지 해상도가 낮고 상세 주소가 누락되었습니다. 수정 후 다시 확인 부탁드립니다."
                        style={{
                            width: '100%',
                            padding: '12px',
                            borderRadius: '10px',
                            border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                            fontSize: '13px',
                            boxSizing: 'border-box',
                            outline: 'none',
                            resize: 'vertical',
                            lineHeight: 1.5,
                            marginBottom: '18px'
                        }}
                    />

                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: '8px' }}>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={onClose}
                            style={{
                                padding: '10px 18px',
                                borderRadius: '8px',
                                border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                                color: '#475569',
                                fontSize: '13px',
                                fontWeight: 600,
                                cursor: 'pointer'
                            }}
                        >
                            취소
                        </button>
                        <button
                            type="submit"
                            disabled={isProcessing}
                            style={{
                                padding: '10px 20px',
                                borderRadius: '8px',
                                border: 'none',
                                backgroundColor: '#e11d48',
                                color: '#ffffff',
                                fontSize: '13px',
                                fontWeight: 700,
                                cursor: isProcessing ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer'
                            }}
                        >
                            {isProcessing ? '처리 중...' : '반려 확정'}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

                <div style={{
                    backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',

                        신청 파트너(HOST) 정보
                    </span>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '7px', fontSize: '14px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a' }}>

                        <Mail size={14} color="#94a3b8" />
                        <span>{item.hostEmail || '-'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '7px', fontSize: '12px', color: '#334155', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                        <Briefcase size={14} color="#0f766e" />
                        <span>사업자번호: {item.businessNo || '미등록'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{

                        paddingTop: '8px',
                        borderTop: '1px dashed #cbd5e1',
                        fontSize: '11px',
                        color: '#64748b'
                    }}>
                        좌표: X({item.mapX || '-'}) / Y({item.mapY || '-'})
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* 3. 하단 바: 반려 / 승인 액션 버튼 */}
            <div style={{
                padding: '14px 24px',
                borderTop: '1px solid #f1f5f9',
                backgroundColor: '#fcfdfe',
                display: 'flex',
                justifyContent: 'flex-end',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '10px'
            }}>
                <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={isProcessing}
                    onClick={() => onOpenReject(item)}
                    style={{
                        padding: '10px 18px',
                        backgroundColor: '#fff1f2',
                        color: '#e11d48',
                        border: '1px solid #fecdd3',
                        borderRadius: '10px',
                        fontSize: '13px',
                        fontWeight: 700,
                        cursor: isProcessing ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                        display: 'inline-flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: '6px'
                    }}
                >
                    <XCircle size={16} /> 반려 처리
                </button>

                <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={isProcessing}
                    onClick={() => onApprove(item)}
                    style={{
                        padding: '10px 22px',
                        backgroundColor: '#0f766e',
                        color: '#ffffff',
                        border: 'none',
                        borderRadius: '10px',
                        fontSize: '13px',
                        fontWeight: 700,
                        cursor: isProcessing ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                        display: 'inline-flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: '6px',
                        boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(15, 118, 110, 0.2)'
                    }}
                >
                    <CheckCircle2 size={16} />
                    {isProcessing ? '처리 중...' : '숙소 등록 승인'}
                </button>
            </div>
        </article>
    );
}

/// <reference types="vite/client" />


// ==========================================
// File: src/hooks/useReservation.ts
// ==========================================
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { createReservation } from '../api/stays';
import type { Booking, Stay } from '../types/stay';

export function useReservation(apiOnline: boolean) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Stay | null>(null);
  const [checkIn, setCheckIn] = useState('');
  const [checkOut, setCheckOut] = useState('');
  const [guests, setGuests] = useState(2);
  const [guestName, setGuestName] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.style.overflow = selected ? 'hidden' : '';
    return () => { document.body.style.overflow = ''; };
  }, [selected]);

  const open = (stay: Stay) => { setSelected(stay); setFeedback(''); };
  const close = () => { setSelected(null); setFeedback(''); };

  const book = async (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!selected) return;
    if (checkOut <= checkIn) {
      setFeedback('퇴실일은 입실일 다음 날부터 선택해 주세요.');
      return;
    }
    if (!apiOnline) {
      setFeedback('현재 예약 접수를 준비 중입니다.');
      return;
    }
    setSubmitting(true);
    setFeedback('');
    try {
      const booking: Booking = {
        contentId: selected.contentId, // 수정: contentId 바인딩
        checkIn,
        checkOut,
        guests,
        guestName,
        phone
      };
      const data = await createReservation(booking);
      setFeedback(`예약 요청이 접수되었습니다. 예약번호: ${data.reservationCode || data.id}`);
    } catch (error) {
      setFeedback(error instanceof Error ? error.message : '잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return { selected, open, close, checkIn, setCheckIn, checkOut, setCheckOut, guests, setGuests,
    guestName, setGuestName, phone, setPhone, submitting, feedback, book };
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/hooks/useHeroSlider.ts
// ==========================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

const slideCount = 3;

export function useHeroSlider() {
  const [slide, setSlide] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => setSlide(current => (current + 1) % slideCount), 10000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);
  const previous = () => setSlide(current => (current + slideCount - 1) % slideCount);
  const next = () => setSlide(current => (current + 1) % slideCount);
  return { slide, previous, next };
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/hooks/useStays.ts
// ==========================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { fetchStays } from '../data/stays';
import type { Stay } from '../types/stay';

export function useStays() {
  const [region, setRegion] = useState('전체 지역');
  const [type, setType] = useState('전체 숙박');
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [term, setTerm] = useState('');
  const [appliedCheckIn, setAppliedCheckIn] = useState<string>('');
  const [appliedCheckOut, setAppliedCheckOut] = useState<string>('');
  const [appliedGuests, setAppliedGuests] = useState<number | undefined>(undefined); // 💡 추가

  const [stays, setStays] = useState<Stay[]>([]);
  const [apiOnline, setApiOnline] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setLoading(true);

    fetchStays(region, type, term, appliedCheckIn, appliedCheckOut, appliedGuests) // 💡 전달
        .then(data => {
          if (active) {
            setStays(data);
            setApiOnline(true);
          }
        })
        .catch((err) => {
          console.error('숙소 로딩 실패:', err);
          if (active) setApiOnline(false);
        })
        .finally(() => {
          if (active) setLoading(false);
        });

    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [region, type, term, appliedCheckIn, appliedCheckOut, appliedGuests]);

  const selectRegion = (value: string) => {
    setRegion(value);
    document.getElementById('stays')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
  };

  // 💡 inDate, outDate와 함께 guestsCount 수신
  const search = (inDate?: string, outDate?: string, guestsCount?: number) => {
    setTerm(query);
    if (inDate !== undefined) setAppliedCheckIn(inDate);
    if (outDate !== undefined) setAppliedCheckOut(outDate);
    if (guestsCount !== undefined) setAppliedGuests(guestsCount);
    document.getElementById('stays')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  return {
    region,
    setRegion,
    type,
    setType,
    query,
    setQuery,
    result: stays,
    loading,
    apiOnline,
    selectRegion,
    search,
  };
}

// ==========================================
// File: src/api/axiosInterceptor.ts
// ==========================================
import axios from 'axios';

export function setupAxiosInterceptors() {
    // 1. 요청(Request) 가로채기: 통신을 보낼 때마다 헤더에 Access Token 자동 삽입
    axios.interceptors.request.use(
        (config) => {
            const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');

            // 재발급 요청이 아닐 때만 기존 토큰 세팅
            if (token && !config.url?.includes('/auth/refresh')) {
                config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
            }
            return config;
        },
        (error) => Promise.reject(error)
    );

    // 2. 응답(Response) 가로채기: 401 에러 발생 시 Refresh Token으로 자동 갱신
    axios.interceptors.response.use(
        (response) => response, // 성공한 응답은 그냥 통과
        async (error) => {
            const originalRequest = error.config;

            // 에러가 401(권한 없음)이고, 재발급 API 자체 에러가 아니며, 아직 재시도한 적이 없다면!
            if (
                error.response?.status === 401 &&
                !originalRequest.url?.includes('/auth/refresh') &&
                !originalRequest._retry
            ) {
                originalRequest._retry = true; // 무한 루프 방지용 플래그 꽂기

                const refreshToken = localStorage.getItem('refreshToken');

                // 리프레시 토큰조차 없다면 얄짤없이 쫓아냄
                if (!refreshToken) {
                    localStorage.removeItem('accessToken');
                    alert('로그인이 필요합니다.');
                    window.location.href = '/login';
                    return Promise.reject(error);
                }

                try {
                    // 💡 조용히 백엔드에 토큰 재발급 요청
                    const res = await axios.post('/api/v1/auth/refresh', { refreshToken });

                    // 새로 발급받은 토큰들 저장
                    const newAccessToken = res.data.token;
                    const newRefreshToken = res.data.refreshToken;

                    localStorage.setItem('accessToken', newAccessToken);
                    localStorage.setItem('refreshToken', newRefreshToken);

                    // 💡 아까 실패했던 억울한 요청의 헤더를 새 토큰으로 교체 후 다시 실행! (핵심)
                    originalRequest.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${newAccessToken}`;
                    return axios(originalRequest);

                } catch (refreshError) {
                    // 리프레시 토큰마저 만료되었거나 털린 경우 -> 완전 로그아웃
                    localStorage.removeItem('accessToken');
                    localStorage.removeItem('refreshToken');
                    alert('안전한 이용을 위해 다시 로그인해 주세요.');
                    window.location.href = '/login';
                    return Promise.reject(refreshError);
                }
            }
            return Promise.reject(error); // 401이 아니거나 다른 에러면 그대로 에러 발생
        }
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/api/stays.ts
// ==========================================
import type { Booking, BookingResponse, Stay } from '../types/stay';

const apiBase = (import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || '').replace(/\/$/, '');

export async function fetchStays(): Promise<Stay[]> {
  const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/v1/stays`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('숙소 정보를 불러오지 못했습니다.');
  const stays: unknown = await response.json();
  if (!Array.isArray(stays)) throw new Error('숙소 응답 형식이 올바르지 않습니다.');
  return stays as Stay[];
}

export async function createReservation(booking: Booking): Promise<BookingResponse> {
  const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/v1/reservations`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(booking),
  });
  const data: BookingResponse = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.message || '예약 요청을 처리하지 못했습니다.');
  return data;
}

export async function toggleAccommodationLike(contentId: string): Promise<{ liked: boolean; message: string }> {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
  const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/v1/likes/accommodations/${contentId}`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
    },
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('위시리스트 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
  return response.json();
}

// 💡 내 위시리스트 숙소 목록 조회
export async function fetchMyWishlist(checkIn?: string, checkOut?: string, guests?: number): Promise<Stay[]> {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  if (checkIn && checkIn.trim()) params.append('checkIn', checkIn.trim());
  if (checkOut && checkOut.trim()) params.append('checkOut', checkOut.trim());
  if (guests && guests > 0) params.append('guests', String(guests));

  const queryString = params.toString() ? `?${params.toString()}` : '';
  const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/v1/likes/accommodations/my${queryString}`, {
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
    },
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('위시리스트를 불러오지 못했습니다.');
  return response.json();
}

        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
    });
    return res.data;
}

    const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
    const res = await axios.delete(`/api/v1/reviews/${reviewId}/reply`, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
    });
    return res.data;
}

import axios from 'axios';

export interface SignupPayload {
    email: string;
    password: string;
    name: string;
    businessNo?: string | null;
}

export interface LoginPayload {
    email: string;
    password: string;
}

export interface TokenResponse {
    token: string;
    refreshToken: string;
}

// 회원가입
export const requestSignup = async (payload: SignupPayload): Promise<string> => {
    const response = await axios.post<string>('/api/v1/auth/signup', payload);
    return response.data;
};

// 일반 로그인
export const requestLogin = async (payload: LoginPayload): Promise<TokenResponse> => {
    const response = await axios.post<TokenResponse>('/api/v1/auth/login', payload);
    return response.data;
};

// 카카오 로그인 인증창 URL
const KAKAO_CLIENT_ID = import.meta.env.VITE_KAKAO_CLIENT_ID;

const getRedirectUri = () => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && (window.location.hostname === 'localhost' || window.location.hostname === '127.0.0.1')) {
        return 'http://localhost:8080/api/v1/auth/kakao/callback';
    }
    return `${window.location.origin}/api/v1/auth/kakao/callback`;
};

export const KAKAO_AUTH_URL = `https://kauth.kakao.com/oauth/authorize?client_id=${KAKAO_CLIENT_ID}&redirect_uri=${getRedirectUri()}&response_type=code&prompt=login`;

// ⭐️ [누락되었던 부분 추가] 숙소 관리자(호스트) 신청 API
export const requestApplyHost = async (businessNo: string): Promise<TokenResponse> => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
    const response = await axios.post<TokenResponse>(
        '/api/v1/auth/apply-host',
        { businessNo },
        {
            headers: {
                Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
            },
        }
    );
    return response.data;
};
