import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { requestLogin, KAKAO_AUTH_URL } from '../../api/auth.ts';
import axios from 'axios';
import { Footer } from "../../components/common/Footer.tsx";

export function LoginPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('');

    const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setErrorMessage('');
        setLoading(true);

        try {
            const data = await requestLogin({ email, password });
            // 💡 accessToken만 저장하고, refreshToken 저장 로직은 삭제합니다.
            localStorage.setItem('accessToken', data.token);
            alert('로그인되었습니다!');
            navigate('/');
        } catch (err: unknown) {
            if (axios.isAxiosError(err) && err.response) {
                setErrorMessage(err.response.data?.message || '이메일 또는 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
            } else {
                setErrorMessage('서버와 통신할 수 없습니다.');
            }
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    const handleKakaoLogin = () => {
        window.location.href = KAKAO_AUTH_URL;
    };

    return (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', minHeight: '100vh', backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', position: 'relative' }}>

            {/* 💡 왼쪽 상단 홈으로 가는 로고 링크 추가 */}
            <Link
                to="/"
                style={{
                    position: 'absolute',
                    top: '28px',
                    left: '32px',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    gap: '8px',
                    textDecoration: 'none',
                    color: '#123f4d',
                    fontWeight: 800,
                    fontSize: '20px',
                    zIndex: 10
                }}
            >
                <img src="/favicon.png" alt="제주 야호 로고" style={{ width: '32px', height: '32px' }} />
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'baseline', gap: '6px' }}>
                    제주 야호
                    <small style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: '0.05em' }}>
                        JEJU YA-HO
                    </small>
                </div>
            </Link>

            {/* 로그인 박스 중앙 정렬 래퍼 */}
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', padding: '40px 20px' }}>
                <div style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: '420px', padding: '36px 32px', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', borderRadius: '16px', backgroundColor: '#ffffff', boxShadow: '0 4px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05)' }}>
                    <h2 style={{ textAlign: 'center', margin: '0 0 8px 0', color: '#0f172a', fontSize: '24px' }}>로그인</h2>
                    <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: '#64748b', fontSize: '14px', marginBottom: '28px', marginTop: 0 }}>제주 야호 서비스를 이용해보세요.</p>

                    {/* 카카오 로그인 버튼 */}
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={handleKakaoLogin}
                        style={{
                            width: '100%',
                            padding: '14px',
                            backgroundColor: '#FEE500',
                            color: '#000000',
                            border: 'none',
                            borderRadius: '8px',
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            fontSize: '14px',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            gap: '8px',
                            marginBottom: '24px'
                        }}
                    >
                        카카오로 시작하기
                    </button>

                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', margin: '16px 0', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '13px' }}>
                        <div style={{ flex: 1, height: '1px', backgroundColor: '#e2e8f0' }} />
                        <span style={{ padding: '0 12px' }}>또는 이메일로 로그인</span>
                        <div style={{ flex: 1, height: '1px', backgroundColor: '#e2e8f0' }} />
                    </div>

                    {errorMessage && (
                        <div style={{ padding: '12px 14px', backgroundColor: '#fef2f2', color: '#ef4444', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '13px', marginBottom: '16px', border: '1px solid #fecaca' }}>
                            {errorMessage}
                        </div>
                    )}

                    <form onSubmit={handleLogin} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
                        <div>
                            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: '6px', color: '#334155' }}>이메일</label>
                            <input
                                type="email"
                                required
                                value={email}
                                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="example@jeju.com"
                                style={{ width: '100%', padding: '12px 14px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', boxSizing: 'border-box', outline: 'none', fontSize: '14px' }}
                            />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: '6px', color: '#334155' }}>비밀번호</label>
                            <input
                                type="password"
                                required
                                value={password}
                                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="비밀번호 입력"
                                style={{ width: '100%', padding: '12px 14px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', boxSizing: 'border-box', outline: 'none', fontSize: '14px' }}
                            />
                        </div>

                        <button
                            type="submit"
                            disabled={loading}
                            style={{
                                marginTop: '8px',
                                padding: '14px',
                                backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                color: '#ffffff',
                                border: 'none',
                                borderRadius: '8px',
                                fontWeight: 700,
                                fontSize: '15px',
                                cursor: loading ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                            }}
                        >
                            {loading ? '로그인 중...' : '로그인'}
                        </button>
                    </form>

                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '24px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b' }}>
                        아직 계정이 없으신가요? <Link to="/signup" style={{ color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 700, textDecoration: 'none', marginLeft: '6px' }}>회원가입</Link>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '12px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b' }}>
                        비밀번호를 잊으셨나요? <Link to="/find-password" style={{ color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 700, textDecoration: 'none', marginLeft: '6px' }}>비밀번호 찾기</Link>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <Footer />
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/auth/KakaoCallback.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';

export function KakaoCallback() {
    const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    useEffect(() => {
        const token = searchParams.get('token');
        if (token) {
            // 발급된 JWT 토큰을 로컬스토리지에 저장
            localStorage.setItem('accessToken', token);
            alert('카카오 로그인에 성공했습니다!');
            navigate('/'); // 메인 화면으로 이동
        } else {
            alert('로그인에 실패했습니다.');
            navigate('/login');
        }
    }, [searchParams, navigate]);

    return (
        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '100px', fontSize: '18px' }}>
            로그인 처리 중입니다. 잠시만 기다려주세요...
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/auth/FindPasswordPage.tsx
// ==========================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import axios from 'axios';
import { Mail, KeyRound, Lock, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { Footer } from '../../components/common/Footer.tsx';

export function FindPasswordPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    // 진행 단계 (1: 이메일 전송, 2: 인증코드 확인, 3: 새 비밀번호 설정)
    const [step, setStep] = useState<1 | 2 | 3>(1);

    const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
    const [code, setCode] = useState('');
    const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState('');
    const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('');

    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('');
    const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState('');

    // 3분(180초) 타이머 관리
    const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState(180);

    useEffect(() => {
        let timer: number;
        if (step === 2 && timeLeft > 0) {
            timer = setInterval(() => {
                setTimeLeft((prev) => prev - 1);
            }, 1000);
        }
        return () => clearInterval(timer);
    }, [step, timeLeft]);

    // 초를 MM:SS 형식으로 변환
    const formatTime = (seconds: number) => {
        const m = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
        const s = seconds % 60;
        return `${m}:${s < 10 ? '0' : ''}${s}`;
    };

    // 💡 1단계: 인증코드 발송 (이벤트 타입 명시)
    const handleSendCode = async (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement> | React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setErrorMessage('');
        setSuccessMessage('');
        setLoading(true);

        try {
            await axios.post('/api/v1/members/password/code', { email });
            setSuccessMessage('인증 코드가 이메일로 발송되었습니다. 3분 이내에 입력해 주세요.');
            setStep(2);
            setTimeLeft(180);
        } catch (err: any) {
            setErrorMessage(err.response?.data || '가입되지 않은 이메일이거나 발송에 실패했습니다.');
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    // 💡 2단계: 인증코드 검증 (이벤트 타입 명시)
    const handleVerifyCode = async (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setErrorMessage('');
        setSuccessMessage('');

        if (timeLeft <= 0) {
            setErrorMessage('인증 시간이 만료되었습니다. 인증번호를 다시 요청해 주세요.');
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);

        try {
            await axios.post('/api/v1/members/password/verify', { email, code });
            setSuccessMessage('이메일 인증이 완료되었습니다. 새 비밀번호를 입력해 주세요.');
            setStep(3);
        } catch (err: any) {
            setErrorMessage(err.response?.data || '인증 코드가 올바르지 않습니다.');
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    // 💡 3단계: 비밀번호 재설정 (이벤트 타입 명시)
    const handleResetPassword = async (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setErrorMessage('');
        setSuccessMessage('');

        if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) {
            setErrorMessage('비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
            return;
        }

        if (newPassword.length < 4) {
            setErrorMessage('비밀번호는 최소 4자 이상이어야 합니다.');
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);

        try {
            await axios.post('/api/v1/members/password/reset', { email, newPassword });
            alert('비밀번호가 성공적으로 변경되었습니다! 로그인 페이지로 이동합니다.');
            navigate('/login');
        } catch (err: any) {
            setErrorMessage(err.response?.data || '비밀번호 변경 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', minHeight: '100vh', backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', position: 'relative' }}>

            {/* 💡 왼쪽 상단 홈으로 가는 로고 링크 추가 */}
            <Link
                to="/"
                style={{
                    position: 'absolute',
                    top: '28px',
                    left: '32px',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    gap: '8px',
                    textDecoration: 'none',
                    color: '#123f4d',
                    fontWeight: 800,
                    fontSize: '20px',
                    zIndex: 10
                }}
            >
                <img src="/favicon.png" alt="제주 야호 로고" style={{ width: '32px', height: '32px' }} />
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'baseline', gap: '6px' }}>
                    제주 야호
                    <small style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: '0.05em' }}>
                        JEJU YA-HO
                    </small>
                </div>
            </Link>

            {/* 비밀번호 찾기 박스 중앙 정렬 래퍼 */}
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', padding: '40px 20px' }}>
                <div style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: '420px', padding: '36px 32px', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', borderRadius: '16px', backgroundColor: '#ffffff', boxShadow: '0 4px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05)' }}>
                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: '28px' }}>
                        <div style={{ width: '48px', height: '48px', borderRadius: '50%', backgroundColor: '#f0fdfa', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', margin: '0 auto 16px' }}>
                            <KeyRound size={24} color="#0f766e" />
                        </div>
                        <h2 style={{ fontSize: '24px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 8px 0' }}>비밀번호 찾기</h2>
                        <p style={{ color: '#64748b', fontSize: '14px', margin: 0 }}>
                            {step === 1 && '가입 시 사용한 이메일 주소를 입력해 주세요.'}
                            {step === 2 && '메일로 발송된 6자리 인증 코드를 입력해 주세요.'}
                            {step === 3 && '새로 사용할 비밀번호를 입력해 주세요.'}
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    {/* 에러 및 안내 메시지 */}
                    {errorMessage && (
                        <div style={{ padding: '12px 14px', backgroundColor: '#fef2f2', color: '#ef4444', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '13px', marginBottom: '16px', border: '1px solid #fecaca' }}>
                            {errorMessage}
                        </div>
                    )}
                    {successMessage && (
                        <div style={{ padding: '12px 14px', backgroundColor: '#f0fdfa', color: '#0f766e', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '13px', marginBottom: '16px', border: '1px solid #ccfbf1' }}>
                            {successMessage}
                        </div>
                    )}

                    {/* [1단계: 이메일 입력] */}
                    {step === 1 && (
                        <form onSubmit={handleSendCode} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
                            <div>
                                <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#334155', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                    가입 이메일
                                </label>
                                <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                                    <input
                                        type="email"
                                        required
                                        value={email}
                                        onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                                        placeholder="example@jeju.com"
                                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '12px 14px 12px 38px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', boxSizing: 'border-box', fontSize: '14px', outline: 'none' }}
                                    />
                                    <Mail size={16} color="#94a3b8" style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '14px', left: '12px' }} />
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <button
                                type="submit"
                                disabled={loading}
                                style={{
                                    padding: '14px',
                                    backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                    color: '#ffffff',
                                    border: 'none',
                                    borderRadius: '8px',
                                    fontWeight: 700,
                                    fontSize: '15px',
                                    cursor: loading ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                                }}
                            >
                                {loading ? '인증 코드 발송 중...' : '인증 코드 받기'}
                            </button>
                        </form>
                    )}

                    {/* [2단계: 인증코드 입력] */}
                    {step === 2 && (
                        <form onSubmit={handleVerifyCode} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
                            <div>
                                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                    <label style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#334155' }}>
                                        인증 코드 (6자리)
                                    </label>
                                    <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 700, color: timeLeft <= 30 ? '#ef4444' : '#0f766e' }}>
                                        {formatTime(timeLeft)}
                                    </span>
                                </div>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    required
                                    maxLength={6}
                                    value={code}
                                    onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value)}
                                    placeholder="123456"
                                    style={{ width: '100%', padding: '12px 14px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', boxSizing: 'border-box', fontSize: '18px', letterSpacing: '6px', textAlign: 'center', outline: 'none' }}
                                />
                            </div>

                            <button
                                type="submit"
                                disabled={loading || timeLeft <= 0}
                                style={{
                                    padding: '14px',
                                    backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                    color: '#ffffff',
                                    border: 'none',
                                    borderRadius: '8px',
                                    fontWeight: 700,
                                    fontSize: '15px',
                                    cursor: loading || timeLeft <= 0 ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                                }}
                            >
                                {loading ? '확인 중...' : '인증 확인'}
                            </button>

                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={handleSendCode}
                                style={{ background: 'none', border: 'none', color: '#64748b', fontSize: '13px', cursor: 'pointer', textDecoration: 'underline', marginTop: '4px' }}
                            >
                                인증 코드가 오지 않았나요? 재전송하기
                            </button>
                        </form>
                    )}

                    {/* [3단계: 새 비밀번호 설정] */}
                    {step === 3 && (
                        <form onSubmit={handleResetPassword} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
                            <div>
                                <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#334155', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                    새 비밀번호
                                </label>
                                <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                                    <input
                                        type="password"
                                        required
                                        value={newPassword}
                                        onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                                        placeholder="새 비밀번호 입력"
                                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '12px 14px 12px 38px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', boxSizing: 'border-box', fontSize: '14px', outline: 'none' }}
                                    />
                                    <Lock size={16} color="#94a3b8" style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '14px', left: '12px' }} />
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div>
                                <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#334155', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                    새 비밀번호 확인
                                </label>
                                <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                                    <input
                                        type="password"
                                        required
                                        value={confirmPassword}
                                        onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                                        placeholder="새 비밀번호 다시 입력"
                                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '12px 14px 12px 38px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', boxSizing: 'border-box', fontSize: '14px', outline: 'none' }}
                                    />
                                    <CheckCircle2 size={16} color="#94a3b8" style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '14px', left: '12px' }} />
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <button
                                type="submit"
                                disabled={loading}
                                style={{
                                    padding: '14px',
                                    backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                    color: '#ffffff',
                                    border: 'none',
                                    borderRadius: '8px',
                                    fontWeight: 700,
                                    fontSize: '15px',
                                    cursor: loading ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                                    marginTop: '8px'
                                }}
                            >
                                {loading ? '변경 중...' : '비밀번호 변경 완료'}
                            </button>
                        </form>
                    )}

                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '24px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b' }}>
                        비밀번호가 기억나셨나요? <Link to="/login" style={{ color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 700, textDecoration: 'none', marginLeft: '6px' }}>로그인</Link>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <Footer />
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/auth/ProfileEditPage.tsx
// ==========================================
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import axios from 'axios';
import { User, Mail, Lock, Building, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';
import { Footer } from '../../components/common/Footer.tsx';
import { PageHeader} from "../../components/common/PageHeader.tsx";

interface UserProfile {
    email: string;
    name: string;
    role: string;
    businessNo?: string;
    isSocial: boolean;
}

export function ProfileEditPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [profile, setProfile] = useState<UserProfile | null>(null);
    const [name, setName] = useState('');
    const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState('');
    const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState('');
    const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('');

    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    const [withdrawing, setWithdrawing] = useState(false);
    const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('로그인이 필요한 서비스입니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }

        axios.get<UserProfile>('/api/v1/members/me', {
            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
        })
            .then((res) => {
                setProfile(res.data);
                setName(res.data.name);
            })
            .catch(() => {
                alert('회원 정보를 불러오지 못했습니다.');
                navigate('/');
            })
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    }, [navigate]);

    // 회원정보 수정 제출
    const handleUpdate = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setErrorMessage('');

        if (newPassword || confirmPassword) {
            if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) {
                setErrorMessage('새 비밀번호와 비밀번호 확인이 일치하지 않습니다.');
                return;
            }
            if (newPassword.length < 4) {
                setErrorMessage('새 비밀번호는 최소 4자 이상이어야 합니다.');
                return;
            }
            if (!currentPassword) {
                setErrorMessage('비밀번호를 변경하려면 현재 비밀번호를 입력해야 합니다.');
                return;
            }
        }

        setSaving(true);
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');

        try {
            // 💡 1. 타입에 refreshToken 추가
            const res = await axios.put<{ token: string; refreshToken: string }>('/api/v1/members/me', {
                name,
                currentPassword: currentPassword || undefined,
                newPassword: newPassword || undefined,
            }, {
                headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
            });

            // 💡 2. 액세스, 리프레시 토큰 모두 저장!
            localStorage.setItem('accessToken', res.data.token);
            localStorage.setItem('refreshToken', res.data.refreshToken);

            alert('회원정보가 성공적으로 수정되었습니다.');
            window.location.reload();
        } catch (err: any) {
            setErrorMessage(err.response?.data?.message || '회원정보 수정 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
        } finally {
            setSaving(false);
        }
    };

    // 숙소 관리자 탈퇴 (일반 유저 전환)
    const handleDowngradeHost = async () => {
        const confirmAction = window.confirm(
            '정말 숙소 관리자 자격을 해지하시겠습니까?\n해지 시 일반 회원으로 전환되며 등록된 숙소 관리 기능을 이용할 수 없습니다.'
        );
        if (!confirmAction) return;

        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        try {
            // 💡 1. 타입에 refreshToken 추가
            const res = await axios.patch<{ token: string; refreshToken: string }>('/api/v1/members/me/downgrade-host', {}, {
                headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
            });

            // 💡 2. 액세스, 리프레시 토큰 모두 저장!
            localStorage.setItem('accessToken', res.data.token);
            localStorage.setItem('refreshToken', res.data.refreshToken);

            alert('숙소 관리자 자격이 해지되어 일반 회원으로 전환되었습니다.');
            window.location.href = '/';
        } catch (err: any) {
            alert(err.response?.data?.message || '처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
        }
    };

    // 회원 탈퇴 버튼 클릭 (WITHDRAWN 상태 변경 및 이메일/이름 Unknown 처리)
    const handleWithdraw = async () => {
        const confirmWithdraw = window.confirm(
            '정말 제주 야호 회원을 탈퇴하시겠습니까?\n\n' +
            '· 이용 예정인 예약이나 보유하신 숙소가 있는 경우 탈퇴할 수 없습니다.\n' +
            '· 탈퇴 시 계정은 즉시 비활성화 처리되며 로그아웃됩니다.'
        );
        if (!confirmWithdraw) return;

        setWithdrawing(true);
        setErrorMessage('');
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');

        try {
            const res = await axios.delete('/api/v1/members/me', {
                headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
            });

            localStorage.removeItem('accessToken');
            alert(res.data?.message || '회원 탈퇴가 완료되었습니다. 그동안 이용해 주셔서 감사합니다.');
            window.location.href = '/';
        } catch (err: any) {
            alert(
                err.response?.data?.message ||
                err.response?.data ||
                '회원 탈퇴 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.'
            );
        } finally {
            setWithdrawing(false);
        }
    };

    if (loading) {
        return <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '120px 0', color: '#64748b' }}>회원 정보를 불러오는 중입니다...</div>;
    }

    if (!profile) return null;

    return (
        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
            {/* 공통 상단 헤더 */}
            <PageHeader title="회원정보 수정" />

            <main className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '600px', margin: '40px auto', padding: '0 20px', flex: 1, width: '100%' }}>
                <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderRadius: '16px', padding: '32px', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', boxShadow: '0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,0.04)' }}>
                    <h2 style={{ fontSize: '20px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 8px 0' }}>내 정보 관리</h2>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', margin: '0 0 24px 0' }}>프로필 정보를 수정하거나 비밀번호를 변경할 수 있습니다.</p>

                    {errorMessage && (
                        <div style={{ padding: '10px 14px', backgroundColor: '#fde8e8', color: '#dc2626', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '13px', marginBottom: '18px' }}>
                            {errorMessage}
                        </div>
                    )}

                    <form onSubmit={handleUpdate}>
                        {/* 1. 이메일 (회색 처리 & 수정 불가) */}
                        <div style={{ marginBottom: '18px' }}>
                            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#475569', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                이메일 계정
                            </label>
                            <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    value={profile.email}
                                    disabled
                                    readOnly
                                    style={{
                                        width: '100%',
                                        padding: '11px 12px 11px 40px',
                                        backgroundColor: '#e2e8f0',
                                        color: '#64748b',
                                        border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                        borderRadius: '8px',
                                        fontSize: '14px',
                                        boxSizing: 'border-box',
                                        cursor: 'not-allowed',
                                        userSelect: 'none'
                                    }}
                                />
                                <Mail size={17} color="#64748b" style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '13px', left: '13px' }} />
                            </div>
                            <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#94a3b8', display: 'block', marginTop: '4px' }}>
                                이메일은 변경할 수 없는 고유 계정 ID입니다.
                            </span>
                        </div>

                        {/* 2. 이름 */}
                        <div style={{ marginBottom: '22px' }}>
                            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#334155', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                이름
                            </label>
                            <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    required
                                    value={name}
                                    onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                                    placeholder="이름 입력"
                                    style={{ width: '100%', padding: '11px 12px 11px 40px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '14px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                                />
                                <User size={17} color="#94a3b8" style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '13px', left: '13px' }} />
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* 숙소 관리자 사업자 번호 표시 (있을 경우) */}
                        {profile.role === 'ROLE_HOST' && profile.businessNo && (
                            <div style={{ marginBottom: '22px' }}>
                                <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: '#334155', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                                    사업자등록번호
                                </label>
                                <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                                    <input
                                        type="text"
                                        value={profile.businessNo}
                                        disabled
                                        style={{ width: '100%', padding: '11px 12px 11px 40px', backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '14px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                                    />
                                    <Building size={17} color="#94a3b8" style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '13px', left: '13px' }} />
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        )}

                        <div style={{ borderTop: '1px solid #f1f5f9', margin: '24px 0' }} />

                        {/* 3. 비밀번호 변경 영역 (소셜 가입자는 숨김) */}
                        {!profile.isSocial ? (
                            <>
                                <h3 style={{ fontSize: '15px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 12px 0' }}>
                                    비밀번호 변경
                                </h3>
                                <p style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#64748b', margin: '0 0 16px 0' }}>
                                    비밀번호를 변경하지 않으려면 입력칸을 비워두세요.
                                </p>

                                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '14px', marginBottom: '28px' }}>
                                    <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                                        <input
                                            type="password"
                                            value={currentPassword}
                                            onChange={(e) => setCurrentPassword(e.target.value)}
                                            placeholder="현재 비밀번호 입력"
                                            style={{ width: '100%', padding: '11px 12px 11px 40px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '14px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                                        />
                                        <Lock size={17} color="#94a3b8" style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '13px', left: '13px' }} />
                                    </div>
                                    <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                                        <input
                                            type="password"
                                            value={newPassword}
                                            onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                                            placeholder="새 비밀번호 (4자 이상)"
                                            style={{ width: '100%', padding: '11px 12px 11px 40px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '14px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                                        />
                                        <Lock size={17} color="#94a3b8" style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '13px', left: '13px' }} />
                                    </div>
                                    <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                                        <input
                                            type="password"
                                            value={confirmPassword}
                                            onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                                            placeholder="새 비밀번호 확인"
                                            style={{ width: '100%', padding: '11px 12px 11px 40px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '14px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                                        />
                                        <Lock size={17} color="#94a3b8" style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '13px', left: '13px' }} />
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </>
                        ) : (
                            <div style={{ padding: '12px 16px', backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '12px', color: '#64748b', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
                                카카오 소셜 간편 로그인 계정은 비밀번호 변경이 필요하지 않습니다.
                            </div>
                        )}

                        {/* 4. 하단 액션 버튼 배치 영역 */}
                        <div style={{
                            display: 'flex',
                            justifyContent: 'space-between',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            marginTop: '32px',
                            paddingTop: '20px',
                            borderTop: '1px solid #f1f5f9',
                            gap: '12px'
                        }}>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                disabled={withdrawing || saving}
                                onClick={handleWithdraw}
                                style={{
                                    background: 'none',
                                    border: 'none',
                                    color: '#94a3b8',
                                    fontSize: '13px',
                                    cursor: withdrawing || saving ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                                    textDecoration: 'underline',
                                    padding: '6px 0',
                                    whiteSpace: 'nowrap'
                                }}
                            >
                                {withdrawing ? '탈퇴 처리 중...' : '회원탈퇴'}
                            </button>

                            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' }}>
                                {profile.role === 'ROLE_HOST' && (
                                    <button
                                        type="button"
                                        disabled={saving || withdrawing}
                                        onClick={handleDowngradeHost}
                                        style={{
                                            padding: '11px 16px',
                                            backgroundColor: '#fff1f2',
                                            color: '#e11d48',
                                            border: '1px solid #fecdd3',
                                            borderRadius: '8px',
                                            fontSize: '13px',
                                            fontWeight: 600,
                                            cursor: saving || withdrawing ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                                            display: 'flex',
                                            alignItems: 'center',
                                            gap: '5px',
                                            whiteSpace: 'nowrap'
                                        }}
                                    >
                                        <AlertTriangle size={15} /> 숙소 관리자 해지
                                    </button>
                                )}

                                <button
                                    type="submit"
                                    disabled={saving || withdrawing}
                                    style={{
                                        padding: '11px 22px',
                                        backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                        color: '#ffffff',
                                        border: 'none',
                                        borderRadius: '8px',
                                        fontSize: '14px',
                                        fontWeight: 700,
                                        cursor: saving || withdrawing ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                                        whiteSpace: 'nowrap'
                                    }}
                                >
                                    {saving ? '수정 중...' : '회원정보 수정'}
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </form>
                </div>
            </main>

            <Footer />
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/auth/SignupPage.tsx
// ==========================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { requestSignup, KAKAO_AUTH_URL } from '../../api/auth.ts';
import axios from 'axios';
import { Footer } from "../../components/common/Footer.tsx";

export function SignupPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [passwordConfirm, setPasswordConfirm] = useState('');
    const [name, setName] = useState('');
    const [isHost, setIsHost] = useState(false);
    const [businessNo, setBusinessNo] = useState('');

    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('');

    // 💡 폼 이벤트 경고 방지를 위해 <HTMLFormElement> 추가
    const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setErrorMessage('');

        if (password !== passwordConfirm) {
            setErrorMessage('비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
            return;
        }

        if (isHost && !businessNo.trim()) {
            setErrorMessage('호스트 회원은 사업자등록번호를 입력해야 합니다.');
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);

        try {
            await requestSignup({
                email,
                password,
                name,
                businessNo: isHost ? businessNo.trim() : null,
            });

            alert('회원가입이 완료되었습니다! 로그인 페이지로 이동합니다.');
            navigate('/login');
        } catch (err: unknown) {
            if (axios.isAxiosError(err) && err.response) {
                setErrorMessage(err.response.data?.message || '회원가입 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
            } else {
                setErrorMessage('서버와 통신할 수 없습니다.');
            }
        } finally {
            setLoading(false);
        }
    };

    const handleKakaoLogin = () => {
        window.location.href = KAKAO_AUTH_URL;
    };

    return (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', minHeight: '100vh', backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', position: 'relative' }}>

            {/* 💡 왼쪽 상단 홈으로 가는 로고 링크 추가 */}
            <Link
                to="/"
                style={{
                    position: 'absolute',
                    top: '28px',
                    left: '32px',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    gap: '8px',
                    textDecoration: 'none',
                    color: '#123f4d',
                    fontWeight: 800,
                    fontSize: '20px',
                    zIndex: 10
                }}
            >
                <img src="/favicon.png" alt="제주 야호 로고" style={{ width: '32px', height: '32px' }} />
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'baseline', gap: '6px' }}>
                    제주 야호
                    <small style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: '0.05em' }}>
                        JEJU YA-HO
                    </small>
                </div>
            </Link>

            {/* 회원가입 박스 중앙 정렬 래퍼 */}
            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', padding: '40px 20px' }}>
                <div style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: '420px', padding: '36px 32px', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0', borderRadius: '16px', backgroundColor: '#ffffff', boxShadow: '0 4px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05)' }}>
                    <h2 style={{ textAlign: 'center', margin: '0 0 8px 0', color: '#0f172a', fontSize: '24px' }}>회원가입</h2>
                    <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: '#64748b', fontSize: '14px', marginBottom: '28px', marginTop: 0 }}>제주 야호에 오신 것을 환영합니다.</p>

                    {/* 카카오 간편 회원가입/로그인 버튼 */}
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={handleKakaoLogin}
                        style={{
                            width: '100%',
                            padding: '14px',
                            backgroundColor: '#FEE500',
                            color: '#000000',
                            border: 'none',
                            borderRadius: '8px',
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            fontSize: '14px',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            justifyContent: 'center',
                            gap: '8px',
                            marginBottom: '24px'
                        }}
                    >
                        카카오로 3초 만에 시작하기
                    </button>

                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', margin: '16px 0', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '13px' }}>
                        <div style={{ flex: 1, height: '1px', backgroundColor: '#e2e8f0' }} />
                        <span style={{ padding: '0 12px' }}>또는 이메일로 가입</span>
                        <div style={{ flex: 1, height: '1px', backgroundColor: '#e2e8f0' }} />
                    </div>

                    {errorMessage && (
                        <div style={{ padding: '12px 14px', backgroundColor: '#fef2f2', color: '#ef4444', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '13px', marginBottom: '16px', border: '1px solid #fecaca' }}>
                            {errorMessage}
                        </div>
                    )}

                    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
                        <div>
                            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: '6px', color: '#334155' }}>이메일</label>
                            <input
                                type="email"
                                required
                                value={email}
                                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="example@jeju.com"
                                style={{ width: '100%', padding: '12px 14px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', boxSizing: 'border-box', outline: 'none', fontSize: '14px' }}
                            />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: '6px', color: '#334155' }}>비밀번호</label>
                            <input
                                type="password"
                                required
                                value={password}
                                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="비밀번호 입력"
                                style={{ width: '100%', padding: '12px 14px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', boxSizing: 'border-box', outline: 'none', fontSize: '14px' }}
                            />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: '6px', color: '#334155' }}>비밀번호 확인</label>
                            <input
                                type="password"
                                required
                                value={passwordConfirm}
                                onChange={(e) => setPasswordConfirm(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="비밀번호 재입력"
                                style={{ width: '100%', padding: '12px 14px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', boxSizing: 'border-box', outline: 'none', fontSize: '14px' }}
                            />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: '6px', color: '#334155' }}>이름</label>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                required
                                value={name}
                                onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="홍길동"
                                style={{ width: '100%', padding: '12px 14px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', boxSizing: 'border-box', outline: 'none', fontSize: '14px' }}
                            />
                        </div>

                        <div style={{ marginTop: '4px' }}>
                            <label style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', fontSize: '13px', cursor: 'pointer', color: '#334155', fontWeight: 500 }}>
                                <input
                                    type="checkbox"
                                    checked={isHost}
                                    onChange={(e) => setIsHost(e.target.checked)}
                                    style={{ width: '16px', height: '16px', cursor: 'pointer' }}
                                />
                                호스트(숙소 운영자)로 가입하기
                            </label>
                        </div>

                        {isHost && (
                            <div>
                                <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: '6px', color: '#334155' }}>사업자등록번호</label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    required={isHost}
                                    value={businessNo}
                                    onChange={(e) => setBusinessNo(e.target.value)}
                                    placeholder="000-00-00000"
                                    style={{ width: '100%', padding: '12px 14px', border: '1px solid #cbd5e1', borderRadius: '8px', boxSizing: 'border-box', outline: 'none', fontSize: '14px' }}
                                />
                            </div>
                        )}

                        <button
                            type="submit"
                            disabled={loading}
                            style={{
                                marginTop: '8px',
                                padding: '14px',
                                backgroundColor: '#123f4d',
                                color: '#ffffff',
                                border: 'none',
                                borderRadius: '8px',
                                fontWeight: 700,
                                fontSize: '15px',
                                cursor: loading ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                            }}
                        >
                            {loading ? '가입 처리 중...' : '회원가입 완료'}
                        </button>
                    </form>

                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '24px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b' }}>
                        이미 계정이 있으신가요? <Link to="/login" style={{ color: '#0f766e', fontWeight: 700, textDecoration: 'none', marginLeft: '6px' }}>로그인하기</Link>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <Footer />
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/admin/AdminAccommodationsPage.tsx
// ==========================================
import { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import axios from 'axios';
import { FileCheck, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { Footer } from '../../components/common/Footer.tsx';
import { PageHeader} from "../../components/common/PageHeader.tsx";
import { PendingAccommodationCard} from "../../components/admim/PendingAccommodationCard.tsx";
import { AdminRejectModal} from "../../components/admim/AdminRejectModal.tsx";

export interface PendingAccommodationItem {
    contentId: string;
    title: string;
    lodgingType: string;
    tel: string;
    zipcode: string;
    addr: string;
    checkinTime: string;
    checkoutTime: string;
    roomCount: string;
    roomType: string;
    parking: string;
    subFacility: string;
    overview: string;
    firstImage: string;
    firstImage2: string;
    mapX: string;
    mapY: string;
    approvalStatus: string;
    hostName?: string;
    hostEmail?: string;
    businessNo?: string;
    createdAt?: string;
}

export function AdminAccommodationsPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const [pendingList, setPendingList] = useState<PendingAccommodationItem[]>([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [searchKeyword, setSearchKeyword] = useState('');
    const [processingId, setProcessingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [rejectTarget, setRejectTarget] = useState<PendingAccommodationItem | null>(null);

    const loadPendingAccommodations = () => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('관리자 로그인이 필요합니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);
        axios.get<PendingAccommodationItem[]>('/api/v1/admin/accommodations/pending', {
            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
        })
            .then((res) => setPendingList(res.data || []))
            .catch((err) => {
                console.error('대기 숙소 목록 조회 실패:', err);
                alert('관리자 권한이 없거나 목록을 불러오지 못했습니다.');
                navigate('/');
            })
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        window.scrollTo(0, 0);
        loadPendingAccommodations();
    }, []);

    const filteredList = useMemo(() => {
        if (!searchKeyword.trim()) return pendingList;
        const kw = searchKeyword.trim().toLowerCase();
        return pendingList.filter(
            (item) =>
                item.title?.toLowerCase().includes(kw) ||
                item.addr?.toLowerCase().includes(kw) ||
                item.hostName?.toLowerCase().includes(kw) ||
                item.businessNo?.toLowerCase().includes(kw)
        );
    }, [pendingList, searchKeyword]);

    const handleApprove = async (item: PendingAccommodationItem) => {
        const ok = window.confirm(
            `[${item.title}]\n이 숙소의 등록을 승인하시겠습니까?\n승인 즉시 홈페이지 숙소 목록에 정식 노출됩니다.`
        );
        if (!ok) return;

        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        setProcessingId(item.contentId);

        try {
            await axios.patch(
                `/api/v1/admin/accommodations/${item.contentId}/approve`,
                {},
                { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }
            );
            alert(`[${item.title}] 숙소 승인이 완료되었습니다.`);
            setPendingList((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.contentId !== item.contentId));
        } catch (err: any) {
            alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.response?.data || '승인 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
        } finally {
            setProcessingId(null);
        }
    };

    const handleConfirmReject = async (contentId: string, rejectReason: string) => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        setProcessingId(contentId);

        try {
            await axios.patch(
                `/api/v1/admin/accommodations/${contentId}/reject`,
                { rejectReason },
                { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } }
            );
            alert(`[${rejectTarget?.title}] 숙소가 반려 처리되었습니다.\n입력하신 반려 사유가 호스트에게 알림으로 전달됩니다.`);
            setPendingList((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.contentId !== contentId));
            setRejectTarget(null);
        } catch (err: any) {
            alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.response?.data || '반려 처리 중 오류가 발생했습니다.');
        } finally {
            setProcessingId(null);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
            {/* 1. 공통 상단 헤더 */}
            <PageHeader
                title="신규 숙소 등록 심사"
                onBack={() => navigate('/')}
                rightElement={
                    <span style={{
                        fontSize: '12px',
                        backgroundColor: '#fce8e6',
                        color: '#c5221f',
                        padding: '4px 10px',
                        borderRadius: '12px',
                        fontWeight: 700
                    }}>
                        ADMIN 총괄 관리 센터
                    </span>
                }
            />

            <main className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '980px', margin: '28px auto', padding: '0 20px', flex: 1, width: '100%' }}>
                {/* 2. 상단 타이틀 및 검색 바 */}
                <div style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'flex-end',
                    flexWrap: 'wrap',
                    gap: '16px',
                    marginBottom: '24px',
                    paddingBottom: '16px',
                    borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0'
                }}>
                    <div>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f766e', letterSpacing: '0.05em', display: 'block', marginBottom: '4px' }}>
                            ACCOMMODATION APPROVAL REVIEW
                        </span>
                        <h1 style={{ fontSize: '24px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 6px 0' }}>
                            승인 대기 중인 숙소 <span style={{ color: '#0f766e' }}>{pendingList.length}건</span>
                        </h1>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', margin: 0 }}>
                            파트너(호스트)가 신청한 신규 숙소 정보를 검토하고 승인 또는 반려 처리할 수 있습니다.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            value={searchKeyword}
                            onChange={(e) => setSearchKeyword(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="숙소명, 주소, 호스트, 사업자번호 검색"
                            style={{
                                padding: '9px 14px 9px 36px',
                                borderRadius: '20px',
                                border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                                fontSize: '13px',
                                width: '250px',
                                outline: 'none'
                            }}
                        />
                        <Search size={15} color="#94a3b8" style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '13px', top: '11px' }} />
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 3. 심사 대기 숙소 카드 목록 */}
                {loading ? (
                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '80px 0', color: '#94a3b8' }}>
                        심사 대기 중인 숙소 목록을 불러오는 중입니다...
                    </div>
                ) : filteredList.length === 0 ? (
                    <div style={{
                        textAlign: 'center',
                        padding: '80px 20px',
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '16px',
                        border: '1px dashed #cbd5e1'
                    }}>
                        <FileCheck size={46} color="#cbd5e1" style={{ marginBottom: '14px' }} />
                        <h3 style={{ fontSize: '17px', color: '#334155', margin: '0 0 8px 0' }}>
                            현재 심사 대기 중인 신규 숙소가 없습니다.
                        </h3>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#94a3b8', margin: 0 }}>
                            모든 숙소 등록 요청이 처리되었습니다.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px' }}>
                        {filteredList.map((item) => (
                            <PendingAccommodationCard
                                key={item.contentId}
                                item={item}
                                isProcessing={processingId === item.contentId}
                                onApprove={handleApprove}
                                onOpenReject={(target) => setRejectTarget(target)}
                            />
                        ))}
                    </div>
                )}
            </main>

            <Footer />

            {/* 4. 분리된 반려 사유 입력 모달 */}
            <AdminRejectModal
                target={rejectTarget}
                isProcessing={Boolean(rejectTarget && processingId === rejectTarget.contentId)}
                onClose={() => setRejectTarget(null)}
                onConfirmReject={handleConfirmReject}
            />
        </div>
    );
}


// ==========================================
// File: src/pages/admin/AdminPartnersPage.tsx
// ==========================================
import React, { useEffect, useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import axios from 'axios';
import {
    Users,
    Building2,
    BedDouble,
    TrendingUp,
    Search,
    Mail,
    Briefcase
} from 'lucide-react';
import { money } from '../../data/stays.ts';
import { Footer } from '../../components/common/Footer.tsx';
import { PageHeader } from '../../components/common/PageHeader.tsx';

export interface PartnerStatItem {
    memberId: number;
    hostName: string;
    email: string;
    businessNo: string;
    accomCount: number;
    roomCount: number;
    totalRevenue: number;
}

export function AdminPartnersPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [partners, setPartners] = useState<PartnerStatItem[]>([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [searchKeyword, setSearchKeyword] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        window.scrollTo(0, 0);
        const token = localStorage.getItem('accessToken');
        if (!token) {
            alert('관리자 로그인이 필요합니다.');
            navigate('/login');
            return;
        }

        setLoading(true);
        axios.get<PartnerStatItem[]>('/api/v1/admin/partners/stats', {
            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
        })
            .then((res) => setPartners(res.data || []))
            .catch((err) => {
                console.error('파트너 통계 조회 실패:', err);
                alert('목록을 불러오지 못했습니다. 관리자 권한을 확인하세요.');
                navigate('/');
            })
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    }, [navigate]);

    // 대시보드 전체 통계 계산 (한 번의 순회로 전체 요약본 도출)
    const overallStats = useMemo(() => {
        let totalAccom = 0;
        let totalRoom = 0;
        let totalRev = 0;

        partners.forEach(p => {
            totalAccom += p.accomCount;
            totalRoom += p.roomCount;
            totalRev += p.totalRevenue;
        });

        return {
            partnerCount: partners.length,
            totalAccom,
            totalRoom,
            totalRev
        };
    }, [partners]);

    // 검색어 필터링
    const filteredPartners = useMemo(() => {
        if (!searchKeyword.trim()) return partners;
        const kw = searchKeyword.trim().toLowerCase();
        return partners.filter((p) =>
            p.hostName?.toLowerCase().includes(kw) ||
            p.email?.toLowerCase().includes(kw) ||
            p.businessNo?.toLowerCase().includes(kw)
        );
    }, [partners, searchKeyword]);

    return (
        <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', minHeight: '100vh', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
            <PageHeader
                title="파트너 및 매출 관리"
                rightElement={
                    <span style={{
                        fontSize: '12px',
                        backgroundColor: '#fce8e6',
                        color: '#c5221f',
                        padding: '4px 10px',
                        borderRadius: '12px',
                        fontWeight: 700
                    }}>
                        ADMIN 총괄 관리 센터
                    </span>
                }
            />

            <main className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '980px', margin: '28px auto', padding: '0 20px', flex: 1, width: '100%' }}>

                {/* 1. 최상단 사이트 전체 요약 대시보드 */}
                <div style={{
                    display: 'grid',
                    gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(220px, 1fr))',
                    gap: '16px',
                    marginBottom: '28px'
                }}>
                    <div style={statCardStyle}>
                        <div style={statHeaderStyle}>
                            <span style={statTitleStyle}>전체 파트너 수</span>
                            <Users size={18} color="#123f4d" />
                        </div>
                        <strong style={statValueStyle}>{overallStats.partnerCount}명</strong>
                    </div>

                    <div style={statCardStyle}>
                        <div style={statHeaderStyle}>
                            <span style={statTitleStyle}>등록된 숙소 수</span>
                            <Building2 size={18} color="#0f766e" />
                        </div>
                        <strong style={statValueStyle}>{overallStats.totalAccom}곳</strong>
                    </div>

                    <div style={statCardStyle}>
                        <div style={statHeaderStyle}>
                            <span style={statTitleStyle}>운영 중인 객실 수</span>
                            <BedDouble size={18} color="#0f766e" />
                        </div>
                        <strong style={statValueStyle}>{overallStats.totalRoom}개</strong>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ ...statCardStyle, backgroundColor: '#f0fdfa', borderColor: '#ccfbf1' }}>
                        <div style={statHeaderStyle}>
                            <span style={{ ...statTitleStyle, color: '#0f766e' }}>사이트 총 누적 매출</span>
                            <TrendingUp size={18} color="#0f766e" />
                        </div>
                        <strong style={{ ...statValueStyle, color: '#0f766e' }}>{money(overallStats.totalRev)}</strong>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 2. 타이틀 및 검색바 */}
                <div style={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'flex-end',
                    flexWrap: 'wrap',
                    gap: '16px',
                    marginBottom: '24px',
                    paddingBottom: '16px',
                    borderBottom: '1px solid #e2e8f0'
                }}>
                    <div>
                        <h1 style={{ fontSize: '20px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: '0 0 6px 0' }}>
                            파트너별 운영 현황
                        </h1>
                        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#64748b', margin: 0 }}>
                            숙소 관리자들의 숙소 등록 개수 및 누적 매출을 확인합니다.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            value={searchKeyword}
                            onChange={(e) => setSearchKeyword(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="이름, 이메일, 사업자번호 검색"
                            style={{
                                padding: '9px 14px 9px 36px',
                                borderRadius: '20px',
                                border: '1px solid #cbd5e1',
                                backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                                fontSize: '13px',
                                width: '250px',
                                outline: 'none'
                            }}
                        />
                        <Search size={15} color="#94a3b8" style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '13px', top: '11px' }} />
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* 3. 파트너 목록 리스트 */}
                {loading ? (
                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '80px 0', color: '#94a3b8' }}>
                        파트너 목록을 불러오는 중입니다...
                    </div>
                ) : filteredPartners.length === 0 ? (
                    <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '80px 20px', backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderRadius: '16px', border: '1px dashed #cbd5e1' }}>
                        <Users size={46} color="#cbd5e1" style={{ marginBottom: '14px' }} />
                        <h3 style={{ fontSize: '16px', color: '#475569', margin: '0 0 8px 0' }}>
                            등록된 파트너 정보가 없습니다.
                        </h3>
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
                        {filteredPartners.map((partner) => (
                            <article
                                key={partner.memberId}
                                style={{
                                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                                    borderRadius: '16px',
                                    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                                    padding: '20px 24px',
                                    display: 'flex',
                                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                                    alignItems: 'center',
                                    flexWrap: 'wrap',
                                    gap: '20px',
                                    boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03)'
                                }}
                            >
                                {/* 파트너 프로필 정보 */}
                                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px' }}>
                                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' }}>
                                        <h3 style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#0f172a', margin: 0 }}>
                                            {partner.hostName}
                                        </h3>
                                        <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#137333', backgroundColor: '#e6f4ea', padding: '3px 8px', borderRadius: '6px', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                                            HOST ID: #{partner.memberId}
                                        </span>
                                    </div>
                                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '16px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#475569' }}>
                                        <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px' }}>
                                            <Mail size={14} color="#94a3b8" /> {partner.email}
                                        </span>
                                        <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px' }}>
                                            <Briefcase size={14} color="#94a3b8" /> {partner.businessNo || '미등록'}
                                        </span>
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                {/* 파트너 실적 통계 박스 */}
                                <div style={{
                                    display: 'flex',
                                    gap: '24px',
                                    backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                                    padding: '14px 24px',
                                    borderRadius: '12px',
                                    border: '1px solid #f1f5f9'
                                }}>
                                    <div>
                                        <span style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '11px', color: '#64748b', marginBottom: '4px' }}>운영 숙소</span>
                                        <strong style={{ fontSize: '16px', color: '#123f4d' }}>{partner.accomCount}곳</strong>
                                    </div>
                                    <div>
                                        <span style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '11px', color: '#64748b', marginBottom: '4px' }}>보유 객실</span>
                                        <strong style={{ fontSize: '16px', color: '#123f4d' }}>{partner.roomCount}개</strong>
                                    </div>
                                    <div style={{ borderLeft: '1px solid #cbd5e1', paddingLeft: '24px' }}>
                                        <span style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '11px', color: '#64748b', marginBottom: '4px' }}>총 누적 매출</span>
                                        <strong style={{ fontSize: '16px', color: '#0f766e' }}>{money(partner.totalRevenue)}</strong>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </article>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                )}
            </main>

            <Footer />
        </div>
    );
}

const statCardStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    padding: '20px',
    borderRadius: '16px',
    border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
    boxShadow: '0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.03)'
};

const statHeaderStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    display: 'flex',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    marginBottom: '10px'
};

const statTitleStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    fontSize: '13px',
    color: '#64748b',
    fontWeight: 600
};

const statValueStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    fontSize: '26px',
    color: '#0f172a',
    fontWeight: 800
